1→"""Typed tool spec contract for generic language plugin registration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, TypedDict
     6→
     7→
     8→class ToolSpec(TypedDict):
     9→    """Validated detector/fixer tool configuration."""
    10→
    11→    label: str
    12→    cmd: str
    13→    fmt: str
    14→    id: str
    15→    tier: int
    16→    fix_cmd: str | None
    17→
    18→
    19→def normalize_tool_specs(
    20→    raw_tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
    21→    *,
    22→    supported_formats: set[str],
    23→) -> list[ToolSpec]:
    24→    """Validate and normalize generic tool specs before plugin assembly."""
    25→    normalized: list[ToolSpec] = []
    26→    for idx, raw in enumerate(raw_tools):
    27→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
    28→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}] must be an object")
    29→
    30→        label = str(raw.get("label", "")).strip()
    31→        cmd = str(raw.get("cmd", "")).strip()
    32→        fmt = str(raw.get("fmt", "")).strip()
    33→        detector_id = str(raw.get("id", "")).strip()
    34→        tier_raw = raw.get("tier")
    35→
    36→        if not label:
    37→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].label must be a non-empty string")
    38→        if not cmd:
    39→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].cmd must be a non-empty string")
    40→        if not fmt:
    41→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].fmt must be a non-empty string")
    42→        if fmt not in supported_formats:
    43→            allowed = ", ".join(sorted(supported_formats))
    44→            raise ValueError(
    45→                f"tools[{idx}].fmt '{fmt}' is unsupported (expected one of: {allowed})"
    46→            )
    47→        if not detector_id:
    48→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].id must be a non-empty string")
    49→        if isinstance(tier_raw, bool) or not isinstance(tier_raw, int):
    50→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].tier must be an integer")
    51→        if tier_raw < 1 or tier_raw > 4:
    52→            raise ValueError(f"tools[{idx}].tier must be in range 1..4")
    53→
    54→        fix_cmd_raw = raw.get("fix_cmd")
    55→        fix_cmd: str | None = None
    56→        if fix_cmd_raw is not None:
    57→            fix_cmd = str(fix_cmd_raw).strip()
    58→            if not fix_cmd:
    59→                raise ValueError(
    60→                    f"tools[{idx}].fix_cmd must be a non-empty string when provided"
    61→                )
    62→
    63→        normalized.append(
    64→            ToolSpec(
    65→                label=label,
    66→                cmd=cmd,
    67→                fmt=fmt,
    68→                id=detector_id,
    69→                tier=tier_raw,
    70→                fix_cmd=fix_cmd,
    71→            )
    72→        )
    73→    return normalized
    74→
    75→
    76→__all__ = ["ToolSpec", "normalize_tool_specs"]
    77→
```

    75→    }
    76→)
    77→_FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS = frozenset(
    78→    {
    79→        # Explicit delegation surface for LangRun -> LangConfig.
    80→        # New LangConfig fields are intentionally opt-in here.
    81→        "name",
    82→        "extensions",
    83→        "exclusions",
    84→        "default_src",
    85→        "build_dep_graph",
    86→        "entry_patterns",
    87→        "barrel_names",
    88→        "phases",
    89→        "fixers",
    90→        "get_area",
    91→        "detect_commands",
    92→        "extract_functions",
    93→        "boundaries",
    94→        "typecheck_cmd",
    95→        "file_finder",
    96→        "large_threshold",
    97→        "complexity_threshold",
    98→        "default_scan_profile",
    99→        "setting_specs",
   100→        "runtime_option_specs",
   101→        "detect_markers",
   102→        "external_test_dirs",
   103→        "test_file_extensions",
   104→        "review_module_patterns_fn",
   105→        "review_api_surface_fn",
   106→        "review_guidance",
   107→        "review_low_value_pattern",
   108→        "holistic_review_dimensions",
   109→        "migration_pattern_pairs",
   110→        "migration_mixed_extensions",
   111→        "zone_rules",
   112→        "integration_depth",
   113→        "detect_lang_security_detailed",
   114→        "detect_private_imports",
   115→        "normalize_settings",
   116→        "normalize_runtime_options",
   117→        "scan_coverage_prerequisites",
   118→        "set_runtime_context",
   119→    }
   120→)
   121→
   122→
   123→# LangRuntimeContract is defined in base.types to avoid an import cycle.
   124→
   125→
   126→@dataclass
   127→class LangRun(LangRunStateAccessors):
   128→    """Runtime facade over an immutable LangConfig."""
   129→
   130→    config: LangConfig
   131→    state: LangRuntimeState = field(default_factory=LangRuntimeState)
   132→
   133→    def __getattr__(self, name: str):
   134→        if name in _FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS:
   135→            return getattr(self.config, name)
   136→        raise AttributeError(
   137→            f"{self.__class__.__name__!s} has no attribute {name!r}; "
   138→            "access runtime state via explicit LangRun properties"
   139→        )
   140→
   141→    def __dir__(self):
   142→        """Expose LangConfig + mutable runtime fields for discoverability."""
   143→        return (
   144→            list(super().__dir__())
   145→            + sorted(_FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS)
   146→            + sorted(_LANG_RUNTIME_STATE_FIELDS)
   147→        )
   148→
   149→
   150→def _coerce_lang_override(field_name: str, value: object) -> object:
   151→    """Normalize override values to LangRuntimeState-compatible payloads."""
   152→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_DICT_FIELDS:
   153→        return value or {}
   154→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_LIST_FIELDS:
   155→        return value or []
   156→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_INT_FIELDS:
   157→        return int(value or 0)
   158→    if field_name == "review_max_age_days":
   159→        if value is None:
   160→            return None
   161→        return int(value)
   162→    return value
   163→
   164→
   165→def _apply_lang_overrides(runtime: LangRun, overrides: LangRunOverrides) -> None:
   166→    """Apply override bundle to runtime state via one normalized loop."""
   167→    for field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_FIELDS:
   168→        value = getattr(overrides, field_name)
   169→        if value is _UNSET:
   170→            continue
   171→        coerced = _coerce_lang_override(field_name, value)
   172→        if field_name == "review_max_age_days" and coerced is None:
   173→            continue
   174→        setattr(runtime, field_name, coerced)
   175→
   176→
   177→def make_lang_run(
   178→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun,
   179→    overrides: LangRunOverrides | None = None,
   180→) -> LangRun:
   181→    """Build a fresh LangRun for a command invocation."""
   182→
   183→    if isinstance(lang, LangRun):
   184→        runtime = lang
   185→    else:
   186→        runtime = LangRun(config=lang)
   187→        runtime.state.runtime_settings = copy.deepcopy(
   188→            getattr(lang, "_default_runtime_settings", {})
   189→        )
   190→        runtime.state.runtime_options = copy.deepcopy(
   191→            getattr(lang, "_default_runtime_options", {})
   192→        )
   193→
   194→    resolved = overrides if overrides is not None else LangRunOverrides()
   195→    _apply_lang_overrides(runtime, resolved)
   196→    return runtime
   197→
   198→
   199→__all__ = [
   200→    "LangRuntimeContract",
   201→    "LangRun",
   202→    "LangRunOverrides",
   203→    "LangRuntimeState",
   204→    "make_lang_run",
   205→]
   206→

1→"""Tree-sitter phase factories for language plugins.
     2→
     3→Each factory takes a ``TreeSitterLangSpec`` and returns a ``DetectorPhase``.
     4→All phases resolve ``lang.file_finder`` at runtime.
     5→
     6→Used by both generic plugins (via ``generic.py``) and full plugins (C#, Dart,
     7→GDScript, TypeScript) that want tree-sitter-powered detection without
     8→duplicating the phase construction logic.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.smell_contracts import normalize_smell_matches
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase
    20→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    21→
    22→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    23→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
    24→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import TreeSitterLangSpec
    25→
    26→# ── Phase factories ────────────────────────────────────────
    27→
    28→
    29→def make_ast_smells_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
    30→    """Create an AST smells phase: empty catches + unreachable code."""
    31→
    32→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    33→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.smells import (
    34→            detect_empty_catches,
    35→            detect_unreachable_code,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    39→        issues: list[Issue] = []
    40→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    41→
    42→        catches = normalize_smell_matches(
    43→            detect_empty_catches(file_list, spec),
    44→            content_key="type",
    45→            default_content="catch",
    46→        )
    47→        for match in catches:
    48→            issues.append(make_issue(
    49→                "smells", match.file, f"empty_catch::{match.line}",
    50→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    51→                summary=f"Empty {match.content} — swallows errors silently",
    52→            ))
    53→        if catches:
    54→            potentials["empty_catch"] = len(catches)
    55→            log(f"         empty catch blocks: {len(catches)}")
    56→
    57→        unreachable = normalize_smell_matches(
    58→            detect_unreachable_code(file_list, spec),
    59→            content_key="after",
    60→            default_content="return",
    61→        )
    62→        for match in unreachable:
    63→            issues.append(make_issue(
    64→                "smells", match.file, f"unreachable_code::{match.line}",
    65→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    66→                summary=f"Unreachable code after {match.content}",
    67→            ))
    68→        if unreachable:
    69→            potentials["unreachable_code"] = len(unreachable)
    70→            log(f"         unreachable code: {len(unreachable)}")
    71→
    72→        return issues, potentials
    73→
    74→    return DetectorPhase("AST smells", run)
    75→
    76→
    77→def make_cohesion_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
    78→    """Create a responsibility cohesion phase."""
    79→
    80→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    81→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.cohesion import (
    82→            detect_responsibility_cohesion,
    83→        )
    84→
    85→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    86→        issues: list[Issue] = []
    87→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    88→
    89→        entries, checked = detect_responsibility_cohesion(file_list, spec)
    90→        for e in entries:
    91→            families = ", ".join(e["families"][:4])
    92→            issues.append(make_issue(
    93→                "responsibility_cohesion", e["file"],
    94→                f"cohesion::{e['file']}",
    95→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
    96→                summary=(
    97→                    f"{e['component_count']} disconnected function clusters "
    98→                    f"({e['function_count']} functions) — likely mixed responsibilities"
    99→                ),
   100→                detail={
   101→                    "cluster_count": e["component_count"],
   102→                    "family": families,
   103→                    "families": e["families"],
   104→                },
   105→            ))
   106→        if entries:
   107→            potentials["responsibility_cohesion"] = len(entries)
   108→            log(f"         low-cohesion files: {len(entries)}")
   109→
   110→        return issues, potentials
   111→
   112→    return DetectorPhase("Responsibility cohesion", run)
   113→
   114→
   115→def make_unused_imports_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
   116→    """Create an unused imports phase."""
   117→
   118→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   119→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.unused_imports import (
   120→            detect_unused_imports,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
   124→        issues: list[Issue] = []
   125→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
   126→
   127→        entries = detect_unused_imports(file_list, spec)
   128→        for e in entries:
   129→            issues.append(make_issue(
   130→                "unused", e["file"], f"unused_import::{e['line']}",
   131→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
   132→                summary=f"Unused import: {e['name']}",
   133→            ))
   134→        if entries:
   135→            potentials["unused_imports"] = len(entries)
   136→            log(f"         unused imports: {len(entries)}")
   137→
   138→        return issues, potentials
   139→
   140→    return DetectorPhase("Unused imports", run)
   141→
   142→
   143→# ── Convenience: all tree-sitter phases for a named language ──
   144→
   145→
   146→def all_treesitter_phases(spec_name: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
   147→    """Return all tree-sitter-powered phases for a language plugin.
   148→
   149→    Convenience bundle — returns AST smells, cohesion, and (when import
   150→    query exists) unused imports.  Returns [] if tree-sitter-language-pack
   151→    is not installed.
   152→    """
   153→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import get_spec, is_available
   154→
   155→    if not is_available():
   156→        return []
   157→
   158→    spec = get_spec(spec_name)
   159→    if spec is None or not spec.function_query:
   160→        return []
   161→
   162→    phases = [
   163→        make_ast_smells_phase(spec),
   164→        make_cohesion_phase(spec),
   165→    ]
   166→
   167→    if spec.import_query:
   168→        phases.append(make_unused_imports_phase(spec))
   169→
   170→    return phases
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = [
   174→    "all_treesitter_phases",
   175→    "make_ast_smells_phase",
   176→    "make_cohesion_phase",
   177→    "make_unused_imports_phase",
   178→]
   179→

1→"""Generic language plugin system — run external tools, parse output, emit issues.
     2→
     3→Provides `generic_lang()` to register a language plugin from a list of tool specs.
     4→Each tool runs a shell command at scan time, parses the output into issues, and
     5→gracefully degrades when the tool is not installed or times out.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZoneRule
    13→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    14→from .capabilities import (
    15→    SHARED_PHASE_LABELS,
    16→    capability_report,
    17→    generic_zone_rules,
    18→    make_file_finder,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.parsers import (
    21→    PARSERS as _PARSERS,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.parsers import (
    24→    parse_cargo,
    25→    parse_eslint,
    26→    parse_gnu,
    27→    parse_golangci,
    28→    parse_json,
    29→    parse_rubocop,
    30→)
    31→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_factories import (
    32→    make_detect_fn,
    33→    make_tool_phase,
    34→)
    35→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_spec import (
    36→    normalize_tool_specs,
    37→)
    38→from .registration import (
    39→    GenericLangOptions,
    40→    _build_generic_phases,
    41→    _register_generic_tool_specs,
    42→    _resolve_generic_extractors,
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def generic_lang(
    47→    name: str,
    48→    extensions: list[str],
    49→    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
    50→    *,
    51→    options: GenericLangOptions | None = None,
    52→    exclude: list[str] | None = None,
    53→    depth: str = "shallow",
    54→    detect_markers: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    default_src: str = ".",
    56→    treesitter_spec=None,
    57→    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] | None = None,
    58→    test_coverage_module: Any | None = None,
    59→    entry_patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    60→) -> LangConfig:
    61→    """Build and register a generic language plugin from tool specs.
    62→
    63→    Each entry in `tools` is::
    64→
    65→        {"label": str, "cmd": str, "fmt": str, "id": str, "tier": int,
    66→         "fix_cmd": str | None}
    67→
    68→    When ``treesitter_spec`` is provided and ``tree-sitter-language-pack`` is
    69→    installed, the plugin gains function extraction (enables duplicate
    70→    detection), and optionally import analysis (enables coupling/orphan/cycle
    71→    detection and test-coverage analysis) for no additional configuration.
    72→
    73→    Returns the built LangConfig (also registered in the language registry).
    74→    """
    75→    opts = options or GenericLangOptions(
    76→        exclude=exclude,
    77→        depth=depth,
    78→        detect_markers=detect_markers,
    79→        default_src=default_src,
    80→        treesitter_spec=treesitter_spec,
    81→        zone_rules=zone_rules,
    82→        test_coverage_module=test_coverage_module,
    83→        entry_patterns=entry_patterns,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    from desloppify.languages import register_generic_lang
    87→
    88→    tool_specs = normalize_tool_specs(tools, supported_formats=set(_PARSERS))
    89→    fixers = _register_generic_tool_specs(tool_specs)
    90→    file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec = _resolve_generic_extractors(
    91→        path_extensions=extensions,
    92→        opts=opts,
    93→    )
    94→    phases = _build_generic_phases(
    95→        tool_specs=tool_specs,
    96→        ts_spec=ts_spec,
    97→        has_treesitter=has_treesitter,
    98→        extract_fn=extract_fn,
    99→        dep_graph_fn=dep_graph_fn,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    cfg = LangConfig(
   103→        name=name,
   104→        extensions=extensions,
   105→        exclusions=opts.exclude or [],
   106→        default_src=opts.default_src,
   107→        build_dep_graph=dep_graph_fn,
   108→        entry_patterns=opts.entry_patterns or [],
   109→        barrel_names=set(),
   110→        phases=phases,
   111→        fixers=fixers,
   112→        get_area=None,
   113→        detect_commands={
   114→            tool["id"]: make_detect_fn(tool["cmd"], _PARSERS[tool["fmt"]])
   115→            for tool in tool_specs
   116→        },
   117→        extract_functions=extract_fn,
   118→        boundaries=[],
   119→        typecheck_cmd="",
   120→        file_finder=file_finder,
   121→        large_threshold=500,
   122→        complexity_threshold=15,
   123→        default_scan_profile="objective",
   124→        detect_markers=opts.detect_markers or [],
   125→        external_test_dirs=["tests", "test"],
   126→        test_file_extensions=extensions,
   127→        zone_rules=opts.zone_rules if opts.zone_rules is not None else generic_zone_rules(extensions),
   128→    )
   129→
   130→    # Set integration depth — upgrade when tree-sitter provides capabilities.
   131→    if has_treesitter and opts.depth in ("shallow", "minimal"):
   132→        cfg.integration_depth = "standard"
   133→    else:
   134→        cfg.integration_depth = opts.depth
   135→
   136→    # Register language-specific test coverage hooks if provided.
   137→    if opts.test_coverage_module is not None:
   138→        from desloppify.languages._framework.registry.state import register_lang_hooks
   139→
   140→        register_lang_hooks(name, test_coverage=opts.test_coverage_module)
   141→
   142→    register_generic_lang(name, cfg)
   143→    return cfg
   144→
   145→
   146→__all__ = [
   147→    "GenericLangOptions",
   148→    "SHARED_PHASE_LABELS",
   149→    "capability_report",
   150→    "generic_lang",

1→"""Detector/fixer factory helpers for generic language plugins."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import subprocess  # nosec B404
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import Any
     9→
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import (
    11→    DetectorPhase,
    12→    FixerConfig,
    13→    FixResult,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.parsers import PARSERS
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_runner import (
    17→    SubprocessRun,
    18→    ToolRunResult,
    19→    resolve_command_argv,
    20→    run_tool_result,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_spec import ToolSpec
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    24→
    25→
    26→def _record_tool_failure_coverage(
    27→    lang: Any,
    28→    *,
    29→    detector: str,
    30→    label: str,
    31→    result: ToolRunResult,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Attach reduced-coverage metadata when generic detector tooling fails."""
    34→    if result.status != "error":
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    record = {
    38→        "detector": detector,
    39→        "status": "reduced",
    40→        "confidence": 0.0,
    41→        "summary": f"{label} tooling unavailable ({result.error_kind or 'error'})",
    42→        "impact": "Detector results may be under-reported for this scan.",
    43→        "remediation": "Install/fix the tool command and rerun scan.",
    44→        "tool": label,
    45→        "reason": result.error_kind or "tool_error",
    46→    }
    47→    detector_coverage = getattr(lang, "detector_coverage", None)
    48→    if isinstance(detector_coverage, dict):
    49→        detector_coverage[detector] = dict(record)
    50→
    51→    coverage_warnings = getattr(lang, "coverage_warnings", None)
    52→    if isinstance(coverage_warnings, list):
    53→        if not any(
    54→            isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("detector") == detector
    55→            for entry in coverage_warnings
    56→        ):
    57→            coverage_warnings.append(dict(record))
    58→
    59→
    60→def make_tool_phase(
    61→    label: str,
    62→    cmd: str,
    63→    fmt: str,
    64→    smell_id: str,
    65→    tier: int,
    66→) -> DetectorPhase:
    67→    """Create a DetectorPhase that runs an external tool and parses output."""
    68→    parser = PARSERS[fmt]
    69→
    70→    def run(path: Path, lang: Any) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int]]:
    71→        run_result = run_tool_result(cmd, path, parser)
    72→        if run_result.status == "error":
    73→            _record_tool_failure_coverage(
    74→                lang,
    75→                detector=smell_id,
    76→                label=label,
    77→                result=run_result,
    78→            )
    79→            return [], {}
    80→        entries = list(run_result.entries)
    81→        if not entries:
    82→            return [], {}
    83→        issues = [
    84→            make_issue(
    85→                smell_id,
    86→                entry["file"],
    87→                f"{smell_id}::{entry['line']}",
    88→                tier=tier,
    89→                confidence="medium",
    90→                summary=entry["message"],
    91→            )
    92→            for entry in entries
    93→        ]
    94→        return issues, {smell_id: len(entries)}
    95→
    96→    return DetectorPhase(label, run)
    97→
    98→
    99→def make_detect_fn(
   100→    cmd: str,
   101→    parser: Callable[[str, Path], list[dict[str, Any]]],
   102→    *,
   103→    run_subprocess: SubprocessRun | None = None,
   104→) -> Callable:
   105→    """Create detect function that runs a tool with an optional injected runner."""
   106→
   107→    def detect(path: Path, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   108→        del kwargs
   109→        result = run_tool_result(cmd, path, parser, run_subprocess=run_subprocess)
   110→        return list(result.entries)
   111→
   112→    return detect
   113→
   114→
   115→def make_generic_fixer(
   116→    tool: ToolSpec,
   117→    *,
   118→    run_subprocess: SubprocessRun | None = None,
   119→) -> FixerConfig:
   120→    """Create a FixerConfig from a tool spec with an optional injected runner."""
   121→    smell_id = tool["id"]
   122→    fix_cmd = tool["fix_cmd"]
   123→    if fix_cmd is None:
   124→        raise ValueError("make_generic_fixer requires tool['fix_cmd'] to be provided")
   125→    detect = make_detect_fn(
   126→        tool["cmd"],
   127→        PARSERS[tool["fmt"]],
   128→        run_subprocess=run_subprocess,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→    def fix(
   132→        entries: list[dict[str, Any]],
   133→        dry_run: bool = False,
   134→        path: Path | None = None,
   135→        **kwargs: Any,
   136→    ) -> FixResult:
   137→        del kwargs
   138→        if dry_run or not path:
   139→            return FixResult(entries=[{"file": e["file"], "line": e["line"]} for e in entries])
   140→        runner = run_subprocess or subprocess.run
   141→        try:
   142→            runner(
   143→                resolve_command_argv(fix_cmd),
   144→                shell=False,
   145→                cwd=str(path),
   146→                capture_output=True,
   147→                text=True,
   148→                timeout=120,
   149→            )
   150→        except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   151→            return FixResult(entries=[], skip_reasons={"tool_unavailable": len(entries)})
   152→        remaining = detect(path)
   153→        fixed_count = max(0, len(entries) - len(remaining))
   154→        return FixResult(
   155→            entries=[{"file": e["file"], "fixed": True} for e in entries[:fixed_count]]
   156→        )
   157→
   158→    return FixerConfig(
   159→        label=f"Fix {tool['label']} issues",
   160→        detect=detect,
   161→        fix=fix,
   162→        detector=smell_id,
   163→        verb="Fixed",
   164→        dry_verb="Would fix",
   165→    )
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "make_detect_fn",
   170→    "make_generic_fixer",
   171→    "make_tool_phase",
   172→]
   173→

    63→def _ts_treesitter_phases() -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    64→    """Cherry-pick tree-sitter phases that complement TS's own detectors."""

    74→
    75→    return [make_cohesion_phase(spec)]

    85→            continue
    86→        functions.extend(extract_ts_functions(filepath))
    87→    return functions

1→"""Zone classification system — deterministic file intent classification.
     2→
     3→Classifies files into zones (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)
     4→based on path patterns. Zone metadata flows through issues, scoring, and the LLM.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→import os
    11→from collections.abc import Callable
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→from enum import Enum
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones_data import (
    17→    CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    18→    SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    19→    SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
    20→    TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    21→)
    22→
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→
    26→class Zone(str, Enum):
    27→    """File intent zone — determines scoring and detection policy."""
    28→
    29→    PRODUCTION = "production"
    30→    TEST = "test"
    31→    CONFIG = "config"
    32→    GENERATED = "generated"
    33→    SCRIPT = "script"
    34→    VENDOR = "vendor"
    35→
    36→
    37→# Zones excluded from the health score
    38→EXCLUDED_ZONES = {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}
    39→
    40→# String values for quick lookup in scoring (issues store zone as string)
    41→EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES = {z.value for z in EXCLUDED_ZONES}
    42→
    43→# Shared review filtering sets (enum-backed to keep comparisons type-safe).
    44→REVIEW_SELECTION_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset({Zone.TEST, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR})
    45→REVIEW_COVERAGE_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset(
    46→    {Zone.TEST, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.SCRIPT}
    47→)
    48→
    49→
    50→def normalize_zone(zone: object) -> Zone | None:
    51→    """Normalize enum/string/duck-typed zone objects to a ``Zone`` member."""
    52→    if isinstance(zone, Zone):
    53→        return zone
    54→    raw = getattr(zone, "value", zone)
    55→    if isinstance(raw, Zone):
    56→        return raw
    57→    if isinstance(raw, str):
    58→        try:
    59→            return Zone(raw)
    60→        except ValueError:
    61→            return None
    62→    return None
    63→
    64→
    65→def zone_in(zone: object, zones: frozenset[Zone] | set[Zone]) -> bool:
    66→    """Return ``True`` when *zone* resolves to a member of *zones*."""
    67→    normalized = normalize_zone(zone)
    68→    return normalized in zones if normalized is not None else False
    69→
    70→
    71→@dataclass
    72→class ZoneRule:
    73→    """A classification rule: zone + list of path patterns.
    74→
    75→    Patterns are matched against relative file paths. First matching rule wins.
    76→    Pattern types (auto-detected from shape):
    77→      - "/dir/"   → substring match on full path (directory marker)
    78→      - ".ext"    → basename ends-with (suffix/extension, e.g. ".d.ts", ".test.")
    79→      - "prefix_" → basename starts-with (trailing underscore)
    80→      - "name.py" → basename exact match (has extension, no /)
    81→      - fallback  → substring on full path
    82→    """
    83→
    84→    zone: Zone
    85→    patterns: list[str]
    86→
    87→
    88→def _match_pattern(rel_path: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
    89→    """Match a zone pattern against a relative file path.
    90→
    91→    See ZoneRule docstring for pattern type conventions.
    92→    """
    93→    basename = os.path.basename(rel_path)
    94→
    95→    # Directory pattern: "/dir/" → substring on padded path
    96→    if pattern.startswith("/") and pattern.endswith("/"):
    97→        return pattern in ("/" + rel_path + "/")
    98→
    99→    # Suffix/extension pattern: starts with "." → contains on basename

100→    if pattern.startswith("."):
   101→        return pattern in basename
   102→
   103→    # Prefix pattern: ends with "_" → basename starts-with
   104→    if pattern.endswith("_"):
   105→        return basename.startswith(pattern)
   106→
   107→    # Suffix pattern: starts with "_" → basename ends-with (_test.py, _pb2.py)
   108→    if pattern.startswith("_"):
   109→        return basename.endswith(pattern)
   110→
   111→    # Exact basename: has a proper file extension (1-5 chars after last dot),
   112→    # no "/" → exact basename match (config.py, setup.py, conftest.py)
   113→    if "/" not in pattern and "." in pattern:
   114→        ext = pattern.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
   115→        if ext and len(ext) <= 5 and ext.isalnum():
   116→            return basename == pattern
   117→
   118→    # Fallback: substring on full path (vite.config, tsconfig, eslint, etc.)
   119→    return pattern in rel_path
   120→
   121→
   122→# ── Common zone rules (shared across languages) ──────────
   123→
   124→COMMON_ZONE_RULES = [
   125→    ZoneRule(Zone.VENDOR, ["/vendor/", "/third_party/", "/vendored/"]),
   126→    ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, ["/generated/", "/__generated__/"]),
   127→    ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["/tests/", "/test/", "/fixtures/"]),
   128→    ZoneRule(Zone.SCRIPT, ["/scripts/", "/bin/"]),
   129→]
   130→
   131→
   132→def classify_file(
   133→    rel_path: str, rules: list[ZoneRule], overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
   134→) -> Zone:
   135→    """Classify a file by its relative path. Overrides take priority."""
   136→    if overrides:
   137→        override = overrides.get(rel_path)
   138→        if override:
   139→            try:
   140→                return Zone(override)
   141→            except ValueError as exc:
   142→                log_best_effort_failure(
   143→                    logger, f"parse zone override for {rel_path}", exc
   144→                )
   145→    for rule in rules:
   146→        for pattern in rule.patterns:
   147→            if _match_pattern(rel_path, pattern):
   148→                return rule.zone
   149→    return Zone.PRODUCTION
   150→
   151→
   152→class FileZoneMap:
   153→    """Cached zone classification for a set of files.
   154→
   155→    Built once per scan from file list + zone rules.
   156→    """
   157→
   158→    def __init__(
   159→        self,
   160→        files: list[str],
   161→        rules: list[ZoneRule],
   162→        rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] | None = None,
   163→        overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   164→    ):
   165→        """Build a zone map from files, ordered rules, and optional overrides."""
   166→        self._map: dict[str, Zone] = {}
   167→        self._rel_map: dict[str, Zone] = {}
   168→        self._rel_fn = rel_fn
   169→        self._overrides = overrides
   170→        for file_path in files:
   171→            rel_path = rel_fn(file_path) if rel_fn else file_path
   172→            zone = classify_file(rel_path, rules, overrides)
   173→            self._map[file_path] = zone
   174→            self._rel_map[rel_path] = zone
   175→
   176→    def get(self, path: str) -> Zone:
   177→        """Get zone for a file path. Returns PRODUCTION if not classified."""
   178→        direct = self._map.get(path)
   179→        if direct is not None:
   180→            return direct
   181→
   182→        rel_direct = self._rel_map.get(path)
   183→        if rel_direct is not None:
   184→            return rel_direct
   185→
   186→        if self._rel_fn is not None:
   187→            try:
   188→                rel_path = self._rel_fn(path)
   189→            except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   190→                rel_path = path
   191→            rel_zone = self._rel_map.get(rel_path)
   192→            if rel_zone is not None:
   193→                return rel_zone
   194→
   195→        return Zone.PRODUCTION
   196→
   197→    def exclude(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   198→        """Return files NOT in the given zones."""
   199→        zone_set = set(zones)
   200→        return [
   201→            file_path
   202→            for file_path in files
   203→            if self._map.get(file_path, Zone.PRODUCTION) not in zone_set
   204→        ]
   205→
   206→    def include_only(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   207→        """Return files that ARE in the given zones."""
   208→        zone_set = set(zones)
   209→        return [
   210→            file_path
   211→            for file_path in files
   212→            if self._map.get(file_path, Zone.PRODUCTION) in zone_set
   213→        ]
   214→
   215→    def counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
   216→        """Return file count per zone."""
   217→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   218→        for zone in self._map.values():
   219→            counts[zone.value] = counts.get(zone.value, 0) + 1
   220→        return counts
   221→
   222→    def production_count(self) -> int:
   223→        """Count files classified as production."""
   224→        return len(self._map) - self.non_production_count()
   225→
   226→    def non_production_count(self) -> int:
   227→        """Count files in excluded zones (test/config/generated/vendor)."""
   228→        return sum(1 for z in self._map.values() if z in EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   229→
   230→    def all_files(self) -> list[str]:
   231→        """Return all classified file paths."""
   232→        return list(self._map.keys())
   233→
   234→    def items(self) -> list[tuple[str, Zone]]:
   235→        """Return all (path, zone) pairs."""
   236→        return list(self._map.items())
   237→
   238→
   239→# ── Zone detection policies ────────────────────────────────
   240→
   241→
   242→@dataclass
   243→class ZonePolicy:
   244→    """Per-zone detection policy.
   245→
   246→    skip_detectors: detectors to skip entirely for this zone.
   247→    downgrade_detectors: detectors whose confidence is downgraded to "low".
   248→    exclude_from_score: whether issues in this zone are excluded from scoring.
   249→    """

1→"""React Context provider nesting detector."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import re
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import find_tsx_files
    11→
    12→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13→
    14→
    15→def detect_context_nesting(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    16→    """Find deeply nested React Context provider trees (>5 levels in one file)."""
    17→    entries = []
    18→    total_files = 0
    19→    provider_open = re.compile(r"<(\w+Provider)\b(?!.*/>)")
    20→    provider_close = re.compile(r"</(\w+Provider)\s*>")
    21→
    22→    for filepath in find_tsx_files(path):
    23→        total_files += 1
    24→        try:
    25→            p = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
    26→            content = p.read_text()
    27→            lines = content.splitlines()
    28→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    29→            logger.debug(
    30→                "Skipping unreadable TSX file %s in context-nesting pass: %s",
    31→                filepath,
    32→                exc,
    33→            )
    34→            continue
    35→
    36→        depth = 0
    37→        max_depth = 0
    38→        providers_at_max: list[str] = []
    39→        provider_stack: list[str] = []
    40→
    41→        for line in lines:
    42→            for match in provider_open.finditer(line):
    43→                depth += 1
    44→                provider_stack.append(match.group(1))
    45→                if depth > max_depth:
    46→                    max_depth = depth
    47→                    providers_at_max = list(provider_stack)
    48→
    49→            for match in provider_close.finditer(line):
    50→                if provider_stack and provider_stack[-1] == match.group(1):
    51→                    provider_stack.pop()
    52→                    depth -= 1
    53→
    54→        if max_depth > 5:
    55→            entries.append({"file": filepath, "depth": max_depth, "providers": providers_at_max})
    56→
    57→    return sorted(entries, key=lambda e: -e["depth"]), total_files
    58→
    59→
    60→__all__ = ["detect_context_nesting"]
    61→
```

1→"""Shared language-plugin registration helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import inspect
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TypeVar
     8→
     9→from . import state
    10→from ..base.types import LangConfig
    11→from .resolution import make_lang_config
    12→from ..structure_validation import validate_lang_structure
    13→
    14→ConfigType = TypeVar("ConfigType")
    15→
    16→
    17→def register_lang_class(name: str, config_cls: type[ConfigType]) -> None:
    18→    """Register one language class through canonical framework validation flow."""
    19→    register_lang_structure_fn = validate_lang_structure
    20→    module = inspect.getmodule(config_cls)
    21→    if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    22→        register_lang_structure_fn(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    23→    if isinstance(config_cls, type) and issubclass(config_cls, LangConfig):
    24→        cfg = make_lang_config(name, config_cls)
    25→        state.register(name, cfg)
    26→        return
    27→    state.register(name, config_cls)
    28→
    29→
    30→def register_lang_class_with(
    31→    name: str,
    32→    config_cls: type[ConfigType],
    33→    *,
    34→    validate_lang_structure_fn=validate_lang_structure,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    """Register a class with injectable structure validation seam (tests/callers)."""
    37→    module = inspect.getmodule(config_cls)
    38→    if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    39→        validate_lang_structure_fn(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    40→    if isinstance(config_cls, type) and issubclass(config_cls, LangConfig):
    41→        cfg = make_lang_config(name, config_cls)
    42→        state.register(name, cfg)
    43→        return
    44→    state.register(name, config_cls)
    45→
    46→
    47→def register_full_plugin(
    48→    name: str,
    49→    config_cls: type[ConfigType],
    50→    *,
    51→    test_coverage: object,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    """Register a full language plugin with uniform hooks + duplicate guard."""
    54→    state.register_lang_hooks(name, test_coverage=test_coverage)
    55→    if state.is_registered(name):
    56→        return
    57→    register_lang_class(name, config_cls)
    58→
    59→
    60→__all__ = [
    61→    "register_full_plugin",
    62→    "register_lang_class",
    63→    "register_lang_class_with",
    64→]
    65→

    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import is_subjective_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap

    28→class PlanScanOptions:
    29→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""

    33→    profile: str = "full"
    34→
    35→
    36→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    37→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    38→
    39→
    40→def _resolve_lang(
    41→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    42→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    43→    if lang is not None:
    44→        return lang
    45→
    46→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    47→    if detected is None:
    48→        langs = available_langs()
    49→        if not langs:
    50→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    51→        detected = langs[0]
    52→    return get_lang(detected)
    53→
    54→
    55→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    56→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    60→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    61→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    62→    )
    63→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    64→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    65→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    66→    )
    67→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    68→
    69→    report = capability_report(lang)
    70→    if report is not None:
    71→        present, missing = report
    72→        if present:
    73→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    74→        if missing:
    75→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    76→
    77→
    78→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    79→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    80→    phases = lang.phases
    81→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    82→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    83→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    84→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    85→    return phases
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    89→    issues: list[Issue] = []
    90→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    91→
    92→    total = len(phases)
    93→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    94→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    95→        phase_issues, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    96→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    97→        issues.extend(phase_issues)
    98→
    99→    return issues, all_potentials
   100→
   101→
   102→def _stamp_issue_context(issues: list[Issue], lang: LangRun) -> None:
   103→    if not issues:
   104→        return
   105→
   106→    zone_policies = None
   107→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   108→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   109→
   110→    for issue in issues:
   111→        issue["lang"] = lang.name
   112→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(issue.get("file", ""))
   116→        issue["zone"] = zone.value
   117→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   118→        if policy and issue.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   119→            issue["confidence"] = "low"
   120→
   121→
   122→def _generate_issues_from_lang(
   123→    path: Path,
   124→    lang: LangRun,
   125→    *,

   128→    profile: str = "full",

   130→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""

   134→    _stamp_issue_context(issues, lang)
   135→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(issues)} issues")
   136→    return issues, all_potentials

   147→
   148→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, get_project_root())
   149→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)

1→"""Runtime setup helpers for review command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.languages.framework import make_lang_run
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from desloppify.languages.framework import LangConfig, LangRun
    16→
    17→
    18→def setup_lang(
    19→    lang: LangConfig,
    20→    path: Path,
    21→    config: dict[str, Any],
    22→    *,
    23→    make_lang_run_fn: Callable[[LangConfig], LangRun],
    24→    file_zone_map_cls: type[FileZoneMap],
    25→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    26→    log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
    27→) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    28→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph and return (run, files)."""
    29→    lang_run = make_lang_run_fn(lang)
    30→    files: list[str] = []
    31→
    32→    if lang_run.zone_rules and lang_run.file_finder:
    33→        files = lang_run.file_finder(path)
    34→        zone_overrides = config.get("zone_overrides") or None
    35→        lang_run.zone_map = file_zone_map_cls(
    36→            files, lang_run.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel_fn, overrides=zone_overrides
    37→        )
    38→
    39→    if lang_run.build_dep_graph and lang_run.dep_graph is None:
    40→        try:
    41→            lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
    42→        except (
    43→            OSError,
    44→            UnicodeDecodeError,
    45→            ValueError,
    46→            TypeError,
    47→            RuntimeError,
    48→        ) as exc:
    49→            log_fn(f"  dep graph unavailable for review context: {exc}")
    50→
    51→    return lang_run, files
    52→
    53→
    54→def setup_lang_concrete(lang: LangConfig, path: Path, config: dict) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    55→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph using concrete dependencies."""
    56→    return setup_lang(
    57→        lang,
    58→        path,
    59→        config,
    60→        make_lang_run_fn=make_lang_run,
    61→        file_zone_map_cls=FileZoneMap,
    62→        rel_fn=rel,
    63→        log_fn=log,
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→__all__ = ["setup_lang", "setup_lang_concrete"]
    68→

1→"""Registration and phase-assembly helpers for generic language plugins."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DetectorMeta, register_detector
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DetectorScoringPolicy,
    11→    register_scoring_policy,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZoneRule
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, FixerConfig
    15→from .capabilities import (
    16→    empty_dep_graph,
    17→    make_file_finder,
    18→    noop_extract_functions,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_parts.tool_factories import (
    21→    make_generic_fixer,
    22→    make_tool_phase,
    23→)
    24→from .structural import (
    25→    _make_coupling_phase,
    26→    _make_structural_phase,
    27→)
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    31→class GenericLangOptions:
    32→    """Optional configuration bundle for generic language registration."""
    33→
    34→    exclude: list[str] | None = None
    35→    depth: str = "shallow"
    36→    detect_markers: list[str] | None = None
    37→    default_src: str = "."
    38→    treesitter_spec: Any | None = None
    39→    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] | None = None
    40→    test_coverage_module: Any | None = None
    41→    entry_patterns: list[str] | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→def _register_generic_tool_specs(tool_specs: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, FixerConfig]:
    45→    fixers: dict[str, FixerConfig] = {}
    46→    for tool in tool_specs:
    47→        has_fixer = tool.get("fix_cmd") is not None
    48→        fixer_name = tool["id"].replace("_", "-") if has_fixer else ""
    49→        register_detector(
    50→            DetectorMeta(
    51→                name=tool["id"],
    52→                display=tool["label"],
    53→                dimension="Code quality",
    54→                action_type="auto_fix" if has_fixer else "manual_fix",
    55→                guidance=f"review and fix {tool['label']} issues",
    56→                fixers=(fixer_name,) if has_fixer else (),
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        register_scoring_policy(
    60→            DetectorScoringPolicy(
    61→                detector=tool["id"],
    62→                dimension="Code quality",
    63→                tier=tool["tier"],
    64→                file_based=True,
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→        if has_fixer:
    68→            fixers[fixer_name] = make_generic_fixer(tool)
    69→    return fixers
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_generic_extractors(
    73→    *,
    74→    path_extensions: list[str],
    75→    opts: GenericLangOptions,
    76→) -> tuple[Any, Any, Any, bool, Any]:
    77→    file_finder = make_file_finder(path_extensions, opts.exclude)
    78→    extract_fn = noop_extract_functions
    79→    dep_graph_fn = empty_dep_graph
    80→    ts_spec = opts.treesitter_spec
    81→    has_treesitter = False
    82→    if ts_spec is None:
    83→        return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    84→
    85→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import is_available
    86→
    87→    if not is_available():
    88→        return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    89→
    90→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.extractors import make_ts_extractor
    91→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.imports.graph import make_ts_dep_builder
    92→
    93→    has_treesitter = True
    94→    extract_fn = make_ts_extractor(ts_spec, file_finder)
    95→    if ts_spec.import_query and ts_spec.resolve_import:
    96→        dep_graph_fn = make_ts_dep_builder(ts_spec, file_finder)
    97→    return file_finder, extract_fn, dep_graph_fn, has_treesitter, ts_spec
    98→
    99→
   100→def _build_generic_phases(
   101→    *,
   102→    tool_specs: list[dict[str, Any]],
   103→    ts_spec: Any,
   104→    has_treesitter: bool,
   105→    extract_fn,
   106→    dep_graph_fn,
   107→) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
   108→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.phase_builders import (
   109→        detector_phase_security,
   110→        detector_phase_test_coverage,
   111→        shared_subjective_duplicates_tail,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    phases = [
   115→        make_tool_phase(tool["label"], tool["cmd"], tool["fmt"], tool["id"], tool["tier"])
   116→        for tool in tool_specs
   117→    ]
   118→    phases.append(_make_structural_phase(ts_spec if has_treesitter else None))
   119→
   120→    if has_treesitter and ts_spec is not None:
   121→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.phases import (
   122→            make_ast_smells_phase,
   123→            make_cohesion_phase,
   124→            make_unused_imports_phase,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→        phases.append(make_ast_smells_phase(ts_spec))
   128→        phases.append(make_cohesion_phase(ts_spec))
   129→        if ts_spec.import_query:
   130→            phases.append(make_unused_imports_phase(ts_spec))
   131→
   132→    if extract_fn is not noop_extract_functions:
   133→        from desloppify.languages._framework.base.phase_builders import detector_phase_signature
   134→
   135→        phases.append(detector_phase_signature())
   136→
   137→    phases.append(detector_phase_security())
   138→    if dep_graph_fn is not empty_dep_graph:
   139→        phases.append(_make_coupling_phase(dep_graph_fn))
   140→        phases.append(detector_phase_test_coverage())
   141→
   142→    phases.extend(shared_subjective_duplicates_tail())
   143→    return phases
   144→
   145→
   146→__all__ = [
   147→    "GenericLangOptions",
   148→    "_build_generic_phases",
   149→    "_register_generic_tool_specs",
   150→    "_resolve_generic_extractors",

Perfect! Now I have enough information. Let me compile the comprehensive summary:

## Framework Detection and Plugin Architecture Summary

### 1. **DetectorPhase Definition and Usage**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/types.py` (lines 41-52)

```python
@dataclass
class DetectorPhase:
    """A single phase in the scan pipeline.
    
    Each phase runs one or more detectors and returns normalized issues.
    The `run` function handles both detection AND normalization (converting
    raw detector output to issues with tiers/confidence).
    """
    
    label: str
    run: Callable[[Path, LangRuntimeContract], tuple[list[DetectorEntry], dict[str, int]]]
    slow: bool = False
```

- **Usage Pattern:** Phases are collected into `LangConfig.phases` list and executed sequentially in `_run_phases()` during scan
- **Execution Flow:** Each phase's `run()` callback receives the scan path and a `LangRuntimeContract` interface (see below), returns issues + potentials dict
- **Selection:** Phases can be filtered by `slow` flag and phase label (via `is_subjective_phase()` check in scan planning)

---

### 2. **Tree-Sitter Horizontal Layer Pattern**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/` package

The tree-sitter framework provides a **horizontal integration layer** where language-agnostic detectors are defined once and reused across all supported languages.

#### **TreeSitterLangSpec** (types.py):
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TreeSitterLangSpec:
    """Per-language tree-sitter configuration."""
    
    grammar: str                                    # TSQuery grammar name
    function_query: str                             # TSQuery for function extraction
    comment_node_types: frozenset[str]              # AST node types for comments
    string_node_types: frozenset[str] = frozenset() # AST node types for strings
    
    import_query: str = ""                          # TSQuery for imports (optional)
    resolve_import: Callable | None = None          # Import path resolver (optional)
    class_query: str = ""                           # TSQuery for classes (optional)
    log_patterns: tuple[str, ...] = (...)           # Regex patterns for log detection
```

#### **Spec Catalog** (specs/):
- `specs.py`: Central registry mapping language names to TreeSitterLangSpec instances
- `compiled.py`: Specs for compiled languages (Go, Rust, Java, Kotlin, C++, C#, Swift, PHP, Dart, Scala)
- `functional.py`: Specs for functional languages (Clojure, Elixir, Erlang, F#, Haskell, OCaml, Julia)
- `scripting.py`: Specs for scripting languages (Ruby, Bash, Lua, Perl, R, JavaScript, TypeScript, Zig, Nim, GDScript, PowerShell)
- Example (Ruby):
  ```python
  RUBY_SPEC = TreeSitterLangSpec(
      grammar="ruby",
      function_query="""(method name: (identifier) @name) @func...""",
      comment_node_types=frozenset({"comment"}),
      import_query="""(call method: (identifier) @_method...)""",
      resolve_import=resolve_ruby_import,
      class_query="""(class name: (constant) @name) @class""",
      log_patterns=(r"^\s*(?:puts |p |pp |Rails\.logger)",),
  )
  ```

#### **Phase Factories** (treesitter/phases.py):
- `all_treesitter_phases(spec_name: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]`: Convenience bundle returning standardized phases for a language
- `make_ast_smells_phase(spec)`: Detects empty catches + unreachable code
- `make_cohesion_phase(spec)`: Detects low-cohesion files (disconnected function clusters)
- `make_unused_imports_phase(spec)`: Detects unused imports (requires `spec.import_query`)

Each factory creates a `DetectorPhase` that:
1. Resolves `lang.file_finder(path)` at runtime
2. Uses tree-sitter to parse and query code
3. Normalizes matches into `Issue` objects

**Key Pattern:** A single spec definition enables **duplicate detection, function extraction, import analysis, and coupling detection** across any language, no custom code needed.

---

### 3. **generic_lang() - Shallow Plugin System**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/_framework/generic_support/core.py` (lines 46-143)

The `generic_lang()` function provides a quick way to register a language plugin from tool specs:

```python
def generic_lang(
    name: str,
    extensions: list[str],
    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],  # Each: {label, cmd, fmt, id, tier, fix_cmd?}
    *,
    options: GenericLangOptions | None = None,
    exclude: list[str] | None = None,
    depth: str = "shallow",           # "shallow", "standard", or "full"
    detect_markers: list[str] | None = None,
    default_src: str = ".",
    treesitter_spec=None,             # Optional TreeSitterLangSpec
    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] | None = None,
    test_coverage_module: Any | None = None,
    entry_patterns: list[str] | None = None,
) -> LangConfig:
```

#### **Tool Spec Format:**
```python
class ToolSpec(TypedDict):
    label: str           # Display name
    cmd: str             # Shell command to run
    fmt: str             # Output format (gnu, json, eslint, golangci, cargo, rubocop)
    id: str              # Detector ID
    tier: int            # 1-4 severity tier
    fix_cmd: str | None  # Optional fix command
```

#### **Phase Assembly Logic** (`_build_generic_phases`):
1. Creates one phase per tool spec using `make_tool_phase()`
2. Adds structural analysis phase (tree-sitter powered if available)
3. If tree-sitter spec provided: adds AST smells + cohesion + unused imports
4. If function extraction available: adds signature analysis
5. Adds security, coupling (if dep graph), test coverage
6. Appends shared subjective tail (review, boilerplate duplication, duplicates)

#### **Depth Levels:**
- **"shallow"**: Tool specs only, basic structural
- **"standard"**: Upgraded when tree-sitter provides capabilities
- **"full"**: All phases including slow ones (duplicates, boilerplate)

**Key Insight:** Tree-sitter specs can upgrade integration depth automatically via `_resolve_generic_extractors()`.

---

### 4. **Deep Plugins - TypeScript Example**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/typescript/__init__.py`

Deep plugins are class-based with explicit phase composition:

```python
class TypeScriptConfig(LangConfig):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            name="typescript",
            extensions=TS_EXTENSIONS,
            phases=[
                DetectorPhase("Logs", phase_logs),
                DetectorPhase("Unused (tsc)", phase_unused),
                DetectorPhase("Dead exports", phase_exports),
                DetectorPhase("Deprecated", phase_deprecated),
                DetectorPhase("Structural analysis", phase_structural),
                DetectorPhase("Coupling + single-use + patterns + naming", phase_coupling),
                *_ts_treesitter_phases(),  # Cohesion from tree-sitter
                detector_phase_signature(),
                detector_phase_test_coverage(),
                DetectorPhase("Code smells", phase_smells),
                detector_phase_security(),
                *shared_subjective_duplicates_tail(),
            ],
            fixers=get_ts_fixers(),
            # ... rest of config
        )
```

**Key Patterns:**
- Uses custom language-specific phases (e.g., `phase_coupling` from `typescript/phases_coupling.py`)
- Selectively includes tree-sitter phases via `_ts_treesitter_phases()` (cherry-picks cohesion)
- Composes shared phase factories: `detector_phase_signature()`, `detector_phase_test_coverage()`, etc.
- Custom `extract_ts_functions()` enables duplicate detection
- Custom `build_dep_graph()` (TypeScript-specific import resolver) enables coupling/test-coverage detection

---

### 5. **LangRuntimeContract - Explicit Runtime Interface**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/types.py` (lines 55-96)

This is a **Protocol** (duck-typed interface) that detectors receive at runtime:

```python
class LangRuntimeContract(Protocol):
    """Explicit runtime interface consumed by detector phases."""
    
    # Configuration (from LangConfig)
    name: str
    extensions: list[str]
    entry_patterns: list[str]
    barrel_names: set[str]
    external_test_dirs: list[str]
    test_file_extensions: list[str]
    review_low_value_pattern: object | None
    file_finder: FileFinder | None  # Resolver for source files
    extract_functions: FunctionExtractor | None
    get_area: Callable[[str], str] | None
    build_dep_graph: DepGraphBuilder
    detect_lang_security_detailed: Callable[[list[str], FileZoneMap | None], LangSecurityResult]
    detect_private_imports: Callable[[dict, FileZoneMap | None], tuple[list[DetectorEntry], int]]
    large_threshold: int
    complexity_threshold: int
    props_threshold: int
    
    # Runtime state (mutable, built during scan)
    zone_map: FileZoneMap | None
    dep_graph: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None
    complexity_map: dict[str, float]
    review_cache: dict[str, Any]
    review_max_age_days: int
    subjective_assessments: dict[str, Any]
    detector_coverage: dict[str, DetectorCoverageRecord]
    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord]
    
    # Methods for accessing runtime settings/options
    def runtime_setting(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any: ...
    def runtime_option(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any: ...
    def scan_coverage_prerequisites(self) -> list[DetectorCoverageStatus]: ...
```

**Concrete Implementation:** `LangRun` wraps a `LangConfig` + mutable `LangRuntimeState`:
- Created by `make_lang_run(config, overrides?)` 
- Delegates config access via `__getattr__`
- Exposes runtime state via `LangRunStateAccessors` mixin
- Mutation points: `zone_map`, `dep_graph`, coverage records (set by scan framework)

---

### 6. **ToolSpec and make_tool_phase()**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/_framework/generic_parts/tool_spec.py` + `/tool_factories.py`

#### **ToolSpec Validation** (tool_spec.py):
- Normalizes tool definitions from raw dicts
- Validates: non-empty label/cmd/fmt, fmt in supported formats, tier 1-4, id non-empty
- Returns strictly-typed `ToolSpec` TypedDict

#### **make_tool_phase()** (tool_factories.py, lines 60-96):
```python
def make_tool_phase(
    label: str,
    cmd: str,
    fmt: str,
    smell_id: str,
    tier: int,
) -> DetectorPhase:
    """Create a DetectorPhase that runs an external tool and parses output."""
    parser = PARSERS[fmt]  # Selects parser based on fmt
    
    def run(path: Path, lang: Any) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int]]:
        run_result = run_tool_result(cmd, path, parser)
        if run_result.status == "error":
            _record_tool_failure_coverage(...)  # Coverage metadata
            return [], {}
        entries = list(run_result.entries)
        issues = [make_issue(...) for entry in entries]
        return issues, {smell_id: len(entries)}
    
    return DetectorPhase(label, run)
```

**Supported Parsers:**
- `parse_gnu`: For GCC/vet/generic flag-style output
- `parse_json`: For JSON output (golangci, eslint JSON mode)
- `parse_eslint`: For ESLint format
- `parse_cargo`: For Cargo clippy
- `parse_rubocop`: For RuboCop JSON

**make_detect_fn()** (line 99-112):
- Creates a detect function callable from CLI commands
- Wraps tool execution with optional injectable subprocess runner (for testing)

**make_generic_fixer()** (line 115-166):
- Creates a `FixerConfig` from a tool spec with `fix_cmd`
- Detects remaining issues after running fix command
- Returns `FixResult` with fixed entries

---

### 7. **Framework Detection Code**

**Location:** `/desloppify/languages/typescript/detectors/react/`

The TypeScript plugin includes **React-specific detectors** that work within the general framework:

- **context.py**: `detect_context_nesting()` - finds deeply nested React Context provider trees (>5 levels)
- **hook_bloat.py**: `detect_hook_return_bloat()` - finds custom hooks returning objects with >12 fields
- **state_sync.py**: Detects state synchronization issues

**Pattern:** These are **conditional detectors** that run within phases IF React is detected (via file content scans or manifest analysis). They're not framework-agnostic like tree-sitter phases; they're TypeScript-specific hooks that integrate into the phase pipeline.

**Note:** No automatic Next.js/React framework detection exists in the core. Detection is implicit via:
- Package.json presence/content (detect_markers)
- TSX/JSX file discovery
- Import patterns (react, next in dep graph)

---

### 8. **Zone Map and Dep Graph - Exposure & Access**

**Location:** `/desloppify/engine/policy/zones.py` + `/desloppify/app/commands/` + `/desloppify/engine/planning/scan.py`

#### **Zone Map**:
```python
class FileZoneMap:
    """Cached zone classification for a set of files."""
    
    def __init__(self, files: list[str], rules: list[ZoneRule], 
                 rel_fn: Callable, overrides: dict[str, str] | None): ...
    
    def get(self, path: str) -> Zone: ...
    def exclude(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]: ...
    def include_only(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]: ...
    def counts(self) -> dict[str, int]: ...
    def all_files(self) -> list[str]: ...
    def items(self) -> list[tuple[str, Zone]]: ...
```

**Zone Classification** (`zones.py` line 132-149):
- Pattern-based rules (directory, suffix, prefix, exact match, substring)
- First matching rule wins
- Returns `Zone` enum: PRODUCTION, TEST, CONFIG, GENERATED, SCRIPT, VENDOR

**Exposure Points:**
1. **Scan Phase** (`engine/planning/scan.py`):
   ```python
   def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides) -> None:
       if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
           return
       files = lang.file_finder(path)
       lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides)
   ```

2. **Review Context** (`app/commands/review/runtime/setup.py`):
   ```python
   def setup_lang(lang, path, config, ...):
       if lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder:
           files = lang.file_finder(path)
           lang_run.zone_map = FileZoneMap(files, lang.zone_rules, ...)
   ```

3. **Issue Stamping** (`engine/planning/scan.py` line 102-120):
   ```python
   for issue in issues:
       zone = lang.zone_map.get(issue.get("file", ""))
       issue["zone"] = zone.value
       # Apply zone policy: downgrade confidence for test zone issues, etc.
   ```

#### **Dep Graph**:
```python
DepGraphBuilder = Callable[[Path], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]
```

**Structure:** Nested dict mapping file → {imported_file: metadata}

**Exposure Points:**
1. **Scan Phase** (`engine/planning/scan.py`):
   ```python
   # Currently not built in main scan flow
   # But available in review context:
   ```

2. **Review Context** (`app/commands/review/runtime/setup.py`):
   ```python
   if lang_run.build_dep_graph and lang_run.dep_graph is None:
       try:
           lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
       except (...) as exc:
           log_fn(f"  dep graph unavailable for review context: {exc}")
   ```

3. **Coupling Detectors** (`engine/detectors/`):
   - Access `lang.dep_graph` to detect cycles, single-use modules, orphaned files
   - Test coverage detector uses dep graph to map test file → source file

**Key Access Pattern:** Both are set during **runtime** (after LangRun creation), stored in mutable `state` field:
```python
lang_run.zone_map = FileZoneMap(...)
lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
```

Detectors receive the populated `LangRuntimeContract` interface and can read both:
```python
def phase_run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract):
    zone = lang.zone_map.get(file_path)  # Can be None
    graph = lang.dep_graph                 # Can be None
```

---

### **Architecture Summary Diagram:**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                      LangConfig (Static)                     │
│  - name, extensions, exclusions, default_src                │
│  - phases: list[DetectorPhase]  ← CORE OF PLUGIN            │
│  - fixers: dict[str, FixerConfig]                           │
│  - build_dep_graph: Callable                                │
│  - extract_functions: Callable | None                       │
│  - zone_rules: list[ZoneRule]                               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓ (wrapped by)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  LangRun (Ephemeral)                         │
│  - config: LangConfig (immutable)                           │
│  - state: LangRuntimeState (mutable)                        │
│    - zone_map: FileZoneMap | None   ← BUILT AT SCAN TIME   │
│    - dep_graph: dict | None         ← BUILT AT SCAN TIME   │
│    - complexity_map: dict                                   │
│    - review_cache: dict                                     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓ (exposed as)
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            LangRuntimeContract (Protocol)                    │
│  Duck-typed interface that DetectorPhase.run() receives     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

PHASE ASSEMBLY:
  Deep Plugin:       generic_lang():
  ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────┐
  │ TypeScript   │   │ Ruby (shallow plugin)│
  │ Config class │   │ via generic_lang()   │
  │ hard-codes   │   │ Spec list defines:   │
  │ phases list  │   │ - tools (external)   │
  └──────────────┘   │ - treesitter_spec?  │
                     │ Phase assembly auto  │
                     └──────────────────────┘
         ↓ (both use)
  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Shared Phase Factories (horizontal)       │
  │ - detector_phase_signature()              │
  │ - detector_phase_test_coverage()          │
  │ - detector_phase_security()               │
  │ - detector_phase_duplicates()             │
  │ - all_treesitter_phases(spec_name)       │
  └──────────────────────────────────────────┘
         ↓ (plus tree-sitter specs if provided)
  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ TreeSitterLangSpec Catalog                │
  │ - GO_SPEC, RUST_SPEC, TYPESCRIPT_SPEC... │
  │ Each enables: cohesion, AST smells,      │
  │   unused imports, function extraction    │
  └──────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

This is the full framework architecture enabling both deep language-specific plugins (TypeScript) and shallow generic plugins (any language via tool specs + optional tree-sitter).

tool_result Read
```
1→# Queue Lifecycle
     2→
     3→`desloppify` now treats the queue as one explicit cycle:
     4→
     5→`scan -> review -> workflow -> triage -> execute -> scan`
     6→
     7→## Rules
     8→
     9→- `scan` is a first-class queue phase.
    10→- `review` covers two entry paths:
    11→  - initial subjective assessment after a fresh scan
    12→  - post-execution non-objective review work after objective execution drains
    13→- `workflow` covers post-review workflow items such as score communication,
    14→  score import, and plan creation.
    15→- `triage` follows workflow and exposes only triage stages.
    16→- `execute` exposes only objective fix work and execution clusters.
    17→
    18→## Deferred Disposition
    19→
    20→Deferred disposition is part of the `scan` boundary, not a separate cycle
    21→phase. If deferred temporary skips exist, they block the scan step until the
    22→user reactivates or permanently dispositions them.
    23→
    24→## Persisted Phase vs Safety Net
    25→
    26→The current lifecycle phase is persisted in `plan.refresh_state.lifecycle_phase`
    27→for debuggability and normal transitions. Queue assembly still re-resolves the
    28→phase from current visible items as a safety net, so stale saved phase state
    29→cannot hide the correct next step after out-of-band changes.
    30→
    31→## Completion Authority
    32→
    33→Queue-tracked work is completed by explicit user actions such as
    34→`desloppify plan resolve`, `plan skip --permanent`, or `plan reopen`.
    35→
    36→Scans and review imports may:
    37→- add new work
    38→- reopen previously completed work when it reappears
    39→- corroborate an existing manual resolution
    40→
    41→They do not silently complete open queue-tracked issues just because a detector
    42→stopped reporting them in the latest scan.
    43→
```

    79→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(

    84→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    85→    persisted_raw = config.get("exclude", [])

   109→def _project_root_from_state_path(state_path_value: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
   110→    """Infer project root from an explicit state path under ``.desloppify``."""
   111→    if state_path_value in (None, ""):
   112→        return None
   113→    try:
   114→        state_file = Path(state_path_value).resolve()
   115→    except OSError:
   116→        return None
   117→    if state_file.parent.name != ".desloppify":
   118→        return None
   119→    if state_file.name == "state.json" or (
   120→        state_file.name.startswith("state-") and state_file.suffix == ".json"
   121→    ):
   122→        return state_file.parent.parent
   123→    return None
   124→

   126→def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   127→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
   128→
   129→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
   130→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
   131→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
   132→    """
   133→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
   134→        return
   135→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
   136→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
   137→        try:
   138→            state_file = state_path(args)
   139→            if state_file:
   140→                saved = load_state(state_file)
   141→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
   142→                if saved_path:
   143→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
   144→                    return
   145→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   146→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
   147→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   148→    args.path = str(
   149→        get_default_scan_path(
   150→            project_root=runtime_root,
   151→            default_src=lang.default_src if lang else None,
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→

   157→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   158→    config = load_config()
   159→
   160→    state_file = state_path(args)
   161→    state = load_state(state_file)
   162→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   163→
   164→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)

   167→def _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root: Path) -> bool:
   168→    """Return True when *root* appears to be the desloppify source checkout."""
   169→    package_dir = root / "desloppify"
   170→    pyproject = root / "pyproject.toml"
   171→    if not package_dir.is_dir() or not (package_dir / "__init__.py").is_file():
   172→        return False
   173→    if not pyproject.is_file():
   174→        return False
   175→    try:
   176→        text = pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   177→    except OSError:

   179→    return 'name = "desloppify"' in text or "name='desloppify'" in text

   182→def _running_installed_package_from_checkout(
   183→    *,

   185→    module_file: str | Path | None = None,
   186→) -> bool:

   188→    root = (cwd_root or get_project_root()).resolve()
   189→    if not _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root):
   190→        return False
   191→    current_module = Path(module_file or __file__).resolve()
   192→    try:
   193→        current_module.relative_to(root)

   195→    except ValueError:

   203→    root = get_project_root().resolve()

   212→    print(
