```
   215→            "dim",
   216→        ),
   217→        file=sys.stderr,
   218→    )
   219→    print(
   220→        colorize(
   221→            "  Use `python -m desloppify ...` or `./.venv/bin/desloppify ...` here "
   222→            "to run the local checkout instead of the installed package.",
   223→            "dim",
   224→        ),
   225→        file=sys.stderr,
   226→    )
   227→
   228→
   229→def _resolve_handler(command: str) -> CommandHandler:
   230→    """Resolve a CLI command name to its typed handler callable."""
   231→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   232→
   233→
   234→def _handle_help_command(
   235→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   236→    parser: argparse.ArgumentParser,
   237→) -> None:
   238→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   239→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   240→    try:
   241→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   242→    except SystemExit:
   243→        return
   244→
   245→
   246→def main() -> None:
   247→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   248→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   249→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   250→            try:
   251→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   252→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   253→                logger.debug(
   254→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   255→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   256→                )
   257→
   258→    parser = create_parser()
   259→    args = parser.parse_args()
   260→    if not args.command:
   261→        parser.print_help()
   262→        return
   263→    if args.command == "help":
   264→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   265→        return
   266→
   267→    try:
   268→        with runtime_scope() as runtime:
   269→            inferred = _project_root_from_state_path(getattr(args, "state", None))
   270→            if inferred is not None:
   271→                runtime.project_root = inferred
   272→            _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout()
   273→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   274→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   275→
   276→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   277→            handler(args)
   278→    except CommandError as exc:
   279→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   280→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   281→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   282→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   283→        sys.exit(1)
   284→
   285→
   286→if __name__ == "__main__":
   287→    main()
   288→
```

```
1→"""Registry accessors for optional language hook modules consumed by detectors."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.registry import state as registry_state
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def register_lang_hooks(
    15→    lang_name: str,
    16→    *,
    17→    test_coverage: object | None = None,
    18→) -> None:
    19→    """Register optional detector hook modules for a language."""
    20→    registry_state.register_lang_hooks(
    21→        lang_name,
    22→        test_coverage=test_coverage,
    23→    )
    24→
    25→
    26→def _bootstrap_language_module(module: object) -> None:
    27→    """Run optional language-module bootstrap hook(s)."""
    28→    register_hooks_fn = getattr(module, "register_hooks", None)
    29→    if register_hooks_fn is not None:
    30→        if not callable(register_hooks_fn):
    31→            raise TypeError("Language module register_hooks entrypoint must be callable")
    32→        register_hooks_fn()
    33→        return
    34→    register_fn = getattr(module, "register", None)
    35→    if register_fn is None:
    36→        return
    37→    if not callable(register_fn):
    38→        raise TypeError("Language module register entrypoint must be callable")
    39→    register_fn()
    40→
    41→
    42→def get_lang_hook(
    43→    lang_name: str | None,
    44→    hook_name: str,
    45→) -> object | None:
    46→    """Get a previously-registered language hook module, lazy-loading if needed."""
    47→    if not lang_name:
    48→        return None
    49→    hook = registry_state.get_hook(lang_name, hook_name)
    50→    if hook is not None:
    51→        return hook
    52→
    53→    # Lazy bootstrap: import the language module and run its registration.
    54→    module_name = f"desloppify.languages.{lang_name}"
    55→    try:
    56→        module = sys.modules.get(module_name) or importlib.import_module(module_name)
    57→        _bootstrap_language_module(module)
    58→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
    59→        logger.debug("Unable to bootstrap language hooks for %s: %s", lang_name, exc)
    60→        return None
    61→
    62→    return registry_state.get_hook(lang_name, hook_name)
    63→
```

```
1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→import argparse
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    11→    scan_inventory_available,
    12→    scan_metrics_available,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    17→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    18→    state_dir = get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
    19→    if not state_dir.exists():
    20→        return None
    21→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    22→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    23→        return candidates[0]
    24→    return None
    25→
    26→
    27→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    28→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    29→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    30→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    31→
    32→
    33→def state_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path | None:
    34→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    35→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    36→    if path_arg:
    37→        return Path(path_arg)
    38→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    39→    if not lang_name:
    40→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    41→    if lang_name:
    42→        resolved = get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    43→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    44→            return resolved
    45→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    46→        if fallback is not None:
    47→            return fallback
    48→        return resolved
    49→
    50→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    51→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    52→        if fallback is not None:
    53→            return fallback
    54→    return None
    55→
    56→
    57→def require_issue_inventory(state: dict) -> bool:
    58→    """Return True when command consumers can rely on the issue inventory."""
    59→    if not scan_inventory_available(state):
    60→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    61→        return False
    62→    return True
    63→
    64→
    65→def require_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> bool:
    66→    """Return True when real scan-derived metrics are available."""
    67→    if not scan_metrics_available(state):
    68→        print(colorize("No completed scan metrics yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    69→        return False
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→__all__ = [
    74→    "require_issue_inventory",
    75→    "require_scan_metrics",
    76→    "state_path",
    77→]
    78→
```

```
    52→
    53→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    54→    """Print the scan header line."""
    55→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    59→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    60→    lines = [
    61→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    62→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    63→    ]
    64→    print("\n".join(lines))
    65→
    66→
    67→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    68→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints.
    69→
    70→    Side-effect only: conditionally prints scan-mode warnings to stdout.
    71→    All branches may be skipped (full scan, no noise budget hit, no hidden
    72→    issues), so the function can legitimately produce no output.
    73→    """
    74→    if not effective_include_slow:
    75→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.budget_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→    if noise.hidden_total:
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize(
    81→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} issues hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    82→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    83→                "dim",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    89→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    90→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    91→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    92→        return
    93→
    94→    for entry in warnings:
    95→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    96→            continue
    97→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    98→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    99→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   100→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   101→
   102→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   103→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   104→        if impact:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   106→        if remediation:
   107→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   108→
   109→
   110→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   111→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge_impl(state)
   112→
   113→
   114→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   115→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   116→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   117→    try:
   118→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   119→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   120→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   121→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   122→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   123→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   124→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   125→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   126→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   127→        runtime,
   128→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   129→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   130→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   131→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   132→    )
   133→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   134→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   135→        print(
   136→            colorize(
   137→                "  * Subjective reset "
   138→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   139→                "yellow",
   140→            )
   141→        )
   142→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   143→
   144→    issues, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   145→    merge = orchestrator.merge(issues, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   146→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   147→
   148→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   149→
   150→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   151→
   152→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   153→    show_score_delta(
   154→        runtime.state,
   155→        merge.prev_overall,
   156→        merge.prev_objective,
   157→        merge.prev_strict,
   158→        merge.prev_verified,
   159→        target_strict=target_value,
   160→    )
   161→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   162→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   163→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   164→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   165→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   166→
   167→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   168→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   169→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   170→
   171→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   172→        merge.diff,
   173→        runtime.state,
   174→        runtime.lang,
   175→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   176→    )
   177→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   178→
   179→    write_query(
   180→        build_scan_query_payload(
   181→            runtime.state,
   182→            runtime.config,
   183→            runtime.profile,
   184→            merge.diff,
   185→            warnings,
   186→            narrative,
   187→            merge,
   188→            noise,
   189→        ),
   190→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    badge_path, _badge_result = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   194→    print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   195→    auto_update_skill()
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = [
   199→    "cmd_scan",
   200→]
   201→
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.command_runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_scan_metrics
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    13→
    14→from .batch.orchestrator import do_import_run, do_run_batches
    15→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    16→from .importing.cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    17→from .importing.flags import ReviewImportConfig
    18→from .merge import do_merge
    19→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    20→from .prepare import do_prepare
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ReviewOptions:
    25→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    26→
    27→    merge: bool = False
    28→    run_batches: bool = False
    29→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    30→    external_start: bool = False
    31→    external_submit: bool = False
    32→    import_file: str | None = None
    33→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    34→    session_id: str | None = None
    35→    allow_partial: bool = False
    36→    scan_after_import: bool = False
    37→    path: str = "."
    38→    dry_run: bool = False
    39→    manual_override: bool = False
    40→    attested_external: bool = False
    41→    attest: str | None = None
    42→
    43→    @classmethod
    44→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    45→        return cls(
    46→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    47→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    48→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    49→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    50→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    51→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    52→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    53→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    54→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    55→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    56→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    58→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    59→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    60→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    61→        )
    62→
    63→
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    74→            _ = exc
    75→
    76→
    77→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    78→    if lang:
    79→        return
    80→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    84→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    85→    return [
    86→        opts.merge,
    87→        opts.run_batches,
    88→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    89→        opts.external_start,
    90→        opts.external_submit,
    91→        import_mode,
    92→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    93→    ]
    94→
    95→
    96→def _validate_mode_selection(
    97→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    98→    *,
    99→    mode_flags: list[bool],
   100→) -> None:
   101→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   102→        raise CommandError(
   103→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   104→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   105→            exit_code=1,
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   109→        raise CommandError(
   110→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   111→            exit_code=2,
   112→        )
   113→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   114→        raise CommandError(
   115→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   116→            exit_code=2,
   117→        )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _is_default_prepare_mode(opts: ReviewOptions, mode_flags: list[bool]) -> bool:
   121→    """True when the command will run the default prepare flow (no explicit mode)."""
   122→    return not any(mode_flags) and not opts.import_file
   123→
   124→
   125→def _run_review_mode(
   126→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   127→    *,
   128→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   129→    runtime,
   130→    state,
   131→    lang,
   132→    state_file,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if opts.merge:
   135→        do_merge(args)
   136→        return
   137→    if opts.run_batches:
   138→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   139→        do_run_batches(
   140→            args,
   141→            state,
   142→            lang,
   143→            state_file,
   144→            config=runtime.config,
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   148→        do_import_run(
   149→            opts.import_run_dir,
   150→            state,
   151→            lang,
   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   155→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   156→            scan_path=opts.path,
   157→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→    if opts.external_start:
   161→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   162→        do_external_start(
   163→            args,
   164→            state,
   165→            lang,
   166→            config=runtime.config,
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    if opts.external_submit:
   170→        do_external_submit(
   171→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   172→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   173→            state=state,
   174→            lang=lang,
   175→            state_file=state_file,
   176→            config=runtime.config,
   177→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   178→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   179→            scan_path=opts.path,
   180→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   181→        )
   182→        return
   183→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   184→        do_validate_import(
   185→            opts.validate_import_file,
   186→            lang,
   187→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   188→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   189→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   190→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   191→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   192→            ),
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    if opts.import_file:
   197→        do_import(
   198→            opts.import_file,
   199→            state,
   200→            lang,
   201→            state_file,
   202→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   203→                config=runtime.config,
   204→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   205→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   206→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   207→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   208→            ),
   209→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   213→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   214→
   215→
   216→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   217→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   218→    _enable_live_review_output()
   219→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   220→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   223→    _require_lang(lang)
   224→
   225→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   226→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   227→    _validate_mode_selection(
   228→        opts,
   229→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # Default prepare and --run-batches need real scan-backed context rather
   233→    # than reconstructed plan inventory.
   234→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   235→        if not require_scan_metrics(state):
   236→            return
   237→
   238→    _run_review_mode(
   239→        args,
   240→        opts=opts,
   241→        runtime=runtime,
   242→        state=state,
   243→        lang=lang,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→    )
   246→
```

```
    57→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    67→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    68→
    69→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    70→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    73→    if output:
    74→        try:
    75→            from pathlib import Path
    76→            output_path = Path(output)
    77→            if output_path.exists():
    78→                print(colorize(
    79→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    80→                    "red",
    81→                ))
    82→                print(colorize(
    83→                    f"  Delete it first (`desloppify plan reset` or `rm {output}`), "
    84→                    f"or choose a different filename.",
    85→                    "dim",
    86→                ))
    87→                return
    88→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    89→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    90→            print_agent_plan(
    91→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    92→                next_command=next_command,
    93→            )
    94→        except OSError as e:
    95→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    96→    else:
    97→        print(plan_md)
    98→        print()
    99→        print_agent_plan(
   100→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
   101→            next_command=next_command,
   102→        )
   103→
   104→
   105→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   106→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
   107→    cmd_plan_output(args)
   108→
   109→
   110→def _plan_queue_line(state: dict, plan: dict) -> str:
   111→    """Build the queue headline with a resilient fallback."""
   112→    try:
   113→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   114→        return format_queue_headline(breakdown)
   115→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   116→        logger.debug("Plan show fell back to raw queue counts.", exc_info=exc)
   117→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   118→        return f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
   119→
   120→
   121→def _skip_kind_counts(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
   122→    """Return total skipped plus temporary/permanent/false-positive counts."""
   123→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   124→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   125→    kind_counts = {
   126→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   127→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   128→    }
   129→    return (
   130→        total_skipped,
   131→        kind_counts["temporary"],
   132→        kind_counts["permanent"],
   133→        kind_counts["false_positive"],
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _print_cluster_summary(plan: dict, *, active: str | None) -> None:
   138→    """Print cluster counts and focused cluster details."""
   139→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   140→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   141→    if not clusters:
   142→        return
   143→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   144→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   145→        member_count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   146→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   147→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   148→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   149→
   150→
   151→def _print_commit_tracking(plan: dict) -> None:
   152→    """Print commit-tracking summary when enabled and populated."""
   153→    cfg = load_config()
   154→    if not cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   155→        return
   156→    ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   157→    if ct["total"] <= 0:
   158→        return
   159→    pr_num = cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   160→    pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   161→    print(
   162→        f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   163→        f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   164→    )
   165→
   166→
   167→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   168→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
   169→    plan = load_plan()
   170→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   171→
   172→    queue_line = _plan_queue_line(runtime.state, plan)
   173→    total_skipped, temp_count, perm_count, fp_count = _skip_kind_counts(plan)
   174→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   175→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   176→
   177→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   178→
   179→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   180→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   181→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   182→    if total_skipped:
   183→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   184→    else:
   185→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   186→    _print_cluster_summary(plan, active=active)
   187→    if described or noted:
   188→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   189→    if active:
   190→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   191→    if superseded:
   192→        print(f"  Scan drift:       {superseded} (no longer actionable in current state)")
   193→
   194→    _print_commit_tracking(plan)
   195→
   196→
   197→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   198→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   199→    plan = load_plan()
   200→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   201→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   202→    reset_plan(plan)
   203→    append_log_entry(
   204→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   205→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   206→    )
   207→    save_plan(plan)
   208→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   209→
   210→
   211→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   212→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   213→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   214→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   215→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   216→    "promote": cmd_plan_promote,
   217→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   218→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   219→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   220→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   221→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   222→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   223→    "backlog": cmd_plan_backlog,
   224→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   225→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   226→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   227→    "scan-gate": cmd_plan_scan_gate,
   228→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   229→    "policy": cmd_policy_dispatch,
   230→    "repair-state": cmd_plan_repair_state,
   231→}
   232→
   233→
   234→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   235→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   236→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   237→    if plan_action is None:
   238→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   242→    if handler is None:
   243→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   244→        return
   245→    handler(args)
   246→
   247→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   248→
```

```
    64→@contextmanager
    65→def plan_lock(path: Path | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    66→    """Acquire exclusive lock on plan file for read-modify-write safety."""
    67→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    68→    lock_path = plan_path.with_suffix(".lock")
    69→    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY)
    71→    try:
    72→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    73→            import msvcrt
    74→
    75→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
    76→        else:
    77→            import fcntl
    78→
    79→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    80→        yield
    81→    finally:
    82→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    83→            import msvcrt
    84→
    85→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
    86→        else:
    87→            import fcntl
    88→
    89→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    90→        os.close(fd)
```

```
   104→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   105→    work_items: dict[str, WorkItem] = {}
   106→    return {
   107→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   108→        "created": utc_now(),
   109→        "last_scan": None,
   110→        "scan_count": 0,
   111→        "overall_score": 0,
   112→        "objective_score": 0,
   113→        "strict_score": 0,
   114→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   115→        "stats": {},
   116→        "work_items": work_items,
   117→        "issues": work_items,
   118→        "scan_coverage": {},
   119→        "score_confidence": {},
   120→        "scan_metadata": {"source": "empty"},
   121→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   122→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   123→    }
   124→
   125→
   126→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   127→    try:
   128→        parsed = int(value)
   129→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   130→        return default
   131→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   132→
   133→
   134→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
   135→    if old not in d:
   136→        return False
   137→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
   138→    return True
   139→
   140→
   141→def migrate_state_keys(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   142→    """Migrate legacy key names in-place.
   143→
   144→    - ``"findings"`` → ``"work_items"``
   145→    - legacy ``"issues"`` → ``"work_items"``
   146→    - ``dimension_scores[dim]["issues"]`` → ``"failing"``
   147→    """
   148→    state_dict = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   149→    _rename_key(state_dict, "findings", "work_items")
   150→    _rename_key(state_dict, "issues", "work_items")
   151→
   152→    for ds in state_dict.get("dimension_scores", {}).values():
   153→        if isinstance(ds, dict):
   154→            _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   155→
   156→    for entry in state_dict.get("scan_history", []):
   157→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   158→            continue
   159→        _rename_key(entry, "raw_findings", "raw_issues")
   160→        for ds in (entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).values():
   161→            if isinstance(ds, dict):
   162→                _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   163→
   164→
   165→def _normalize_scan_metadata(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   166→    raw_metadata = state.get("scan_metadata")
   167→    metadata = raw_metadata if isinstance(raw_metadata, dict) else {}
   168→
   169→    source = _coerce_scan_source(state, metadata)
   170→    normalized: ScanMetadataModel = {"source": source}
   171→    if source == "plan_reconstruction":
   172→        normalized["plan_queue_available"] = bool(metadata.get("plan_queue_available"))
   173→        work_item_count = metadata.get(
   174→            "reconstructed_work_item_count",
   175→            metadata.get("reconstructed_issue_count", 0),
   176→        )
   177→        if isinstance(work_item_count, int) and not isinstance(work_item_count, bool):
   178→            normalized["reconstructed_issue_count"] = max(0, work_item_count)
   179→        else:
   180→            normalized["reconstructed_issue_count"] = 0
   181→
   182→    state["scan_metadata"] = normalized
   183→
   184→
   185→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   186→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   187→    migrate_state_keys(state)
   188→
   189→    mutable_state = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   190→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   191→        mutable_state.setdefault(key, value)
   192→    version = mutable_state.get("version")
   193→    if not isinstance(version, int):
   194→        mutable_state["version"] = CURRENT_VERSION
   195→    elif version < CURRENT_VERSION:
   196→        mutable_state["version"] = CURRENT_VERSION
   197→
   198→    if not isinstance(state.get("work_items"), dict):
   199→        legacy_items = state.get("issues")
   200→        state["work_items"] = legacy_items if isinstance(legacy_items, dict) else {}
```

```
     2→

    10→
    11→[REDACTED]

    15→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    16→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT = "assessment_postflight"
    17→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    18→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    22→
    23→# Coarse lifecycle names remain valid persisted values for older plan data.
    24→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    25→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    26→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    27→
    28→COARSE_PHASE_MAP = {
    29→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    30→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    31→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    32→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    33→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    34→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    35→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    36→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    37→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    38→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE: LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    39→}
    40→
    41→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(COARSE_PHASE_MAP)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    45→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    46→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    47→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    48→        refresh_state = {}
    49→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    50→    return refresh_state
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _touches_objective_issue(
    58→    *,
    59→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None,
    60→    state: dict[str, object] | None,
    61→) -> bool:
    62→    if issue_ids is None:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    real_issue_ids = [
    66→        issue_id
    67→        for issue_id in issue_ids
    68→        if _is_real_queue_issue(str(issue_id))
    69→    ]
    70→    if not real_issue_ids:
    71→        return False
    72→    if not isinstance(state, dict):
    73→        return True
    74→
    75→    issues = state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})
    76→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    77→        return True
    78→
    79→    objective_seen = False
    80→    for issue_id in real_issue_ids:
    81→        issue = issues.get(issue_id)
    82→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    83→            return True
    84→        if counts_toward_objective_backlog(issue):
    85→            objective_seen = True
    86→    return objective_seen
    87→
    88→
    89→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    90→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    91→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    92→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    93→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    94→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    95→            return phase
    96→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    97→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    98→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    99→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   100→    return None
   101→
   102→
   103→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
   104→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
   105→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
   106→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
   107→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   108→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
   109→        return False
   110→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
   111→    return True
   112→
   113→
   114→def coarse_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel | None) -> str | None:
   115→    """Return the coarse lifecycle phase for persisted fine/coarse plan data."""
   116→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   117→        return None
   118→    phase = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   119→    if phase is None:
   120→        return None
   121→    return COARSE_PHASE_MAP.get(phase)
   122→
   123→
   124→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   125→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   126→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   127→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   128→        return True
   129→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   130→
   131→
   132→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    *,
   135→    scan_count: int | None,
   136→) -> bool:
   137→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   138→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   139→    try:
   140→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   141→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   142→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   143→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   144→        return False
   145→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   146→    return True
   147→
   148→
   149→def subjective_review_completed_for_scan(
   150→    plan: PlanModel,
   151→    *,
   152→    scan_count: int | None,
   153→) -> bool:
   154→    """Return True when postflight subjective review finished for *scan_count*."""
   155→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   156→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   157→        return False
   158→    try:
   159→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   160→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   161→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   162→    return refresh_state.get(_SUBJECTIVE_REVIEW_KEY) == normalized_scan_count
   163→
   164→
   165→def mark_subjective_review_completed(
   166→    plan: PlanModel,
   167→    *,
   168→    scan_count: int | None,
   169→) -> bool:
   170→    """Record that subjective review completed for the current postflight scan."""
   171→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   172→    try:
   173→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   174→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   175→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   176→    if refresh_state.get(_SUBJECTIVE_REVIEW_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   177→        return False
   178→    refresh_state[_SUBJECTIVE_REVIEW_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   179→    return True
   180→
   181→
   182→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   183→    plan: PlanModel,
   184→    *,
   185→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   186→    state: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   187→) -> bool:
   188→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on objective issues."""
   189→    if not _touches_objective_issue(issue_ids=issue_ids, state=state):
   190→        return False
   191→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   192→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   193→        return False
   194→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   195→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   196→    return True
   197→
   198→
   199→__all__ = [
   200→    "COARSE_PHASE_MAP",
   201→    "coarse_lifecycle_phase",
   202→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT",
   203→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   204→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   205→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   206→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   207→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   208→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   209→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   210→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   211→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.dynamic_loaders import load_score_update_module
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    14→    COARSE_PHASE_MAP,
    15→    coarse_lifecycle_phase,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    17→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    18→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    19→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    20→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    23→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    27→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    28→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    29→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress_render import (
    30→    format_plan_delta,
    31→    format_queue_block,
    32→    format_queue_headline,
    33→)
    34→
    35→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    38→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    39→
    40→
    41→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→
    45→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46→class QueueBreakdown:
    47→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    48→
    49→    queue_total: int = 0
    50→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    51→    stale_plan_ordered: int = 0
    52→    skipped: int = 0
    53→    subjective: int = 0
    54→    workflow: int = 0
    55→    lifecycle_phase: str | None = None
    56→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    57→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    58→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    59→
    60→    @property
    61→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    62→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    63→
    64→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    65→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    66→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    67→        """
    68→        if self.lifecycle_phase in {
    69→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    70→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    71→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    72→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    73→        }:
    74→            return 0
    75→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    76→
    77→
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    80→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→
    82→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    83→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    84→
    85→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    86→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    87→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    88→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    89→    """
    90→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    91→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    92→    LIVE = "live"
    93→
    94→
    95→def score_display_mode(
    96→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    97→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    98→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    99→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
   100→
   101→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
   102→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
   103→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
   104→    """
   105→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   106→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   107→    if breakdown is None:
   108→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   109→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN:
   110→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   111→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
   112→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   113→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase in {
   114→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   115→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   116→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   117→    }:
   118→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   119→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
   120→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   121→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   122→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   123→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   124→
   125→
   126→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
   127→    state: dict,
   128→    plan: dict | None = None,
   129→    *,
   130→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
   131→) -> QueueBreakdown:
   132→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
   133→
   134→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   135→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   136→    """
   137→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   138→
   139→    result = build_execution_queue(
   140→        state,
   141→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   142→            status="open",
   143→            count=None,
   144→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   145→            include_skipped=False,
   146→            context=context,
   147→        ),
   148→    )
   149→
   150→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
```

```
1→"""Integration tests for lifecycle transitions through reconcile → work queue.
     2→
     3→Exercises the full lifecycle by walking through each stage:
     4→  scan → initial reviews → objectives → postflight scan → subjective review
     5→  → communicate-score/create-plan → triage
     6→
     7→Between scans, items are completed via ``purge_ids`` (what ``plan resolve``
     8→does) and the queue is re-checked without reconciling.  ``reconcile`` only
     9→runs at scan boundaries — matching the real CLI flow.
    10→"""

    39→     "tier": 2, "confidence": "high"},
    40→    {"id": "obj-2", "detector": "naming", "file": "src/util.py", "status": "open",
    41→     "tier": 3, "confidence": "medium"},
    42→]
    43→
    44→
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→# State / plan helpers
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→
    49→def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    50→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
    51→    return (
    52→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    53→        {
    54→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
    55→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
    56→        },
    57→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    62→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
    63→    return (
    64→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    65→        {
    66→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
    67→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
    68→        },
    69→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _build_state(
    74→    issues: list[dict],
    75→    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    76→    *,
    77→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    78→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    79→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    80→) -> dict:
    81→    work_items = {i["id"]: dict(i) for i in issues}
    82→    state: dict = {
    83→        "work_items": work_items,
    84→        "issues": work_items,
    85→        "scan_count": 1,
    86→        "dimension_scores": {},
    87→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    88→    }
    89→    for display, dim_entry, assessment in dims:
    90→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
    91→        dim_key = dim_entry["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["dimension_key"]
    92→        state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key] = assessment
    93→    if strict_score is not None:
    94→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    95→    if overall_score is not None:
    96→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    97→    if objective_score is not None:
    98→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    99→    return state
   100→
   101→
   102→def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
   103→    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
   104→    saved: list[dict] = []
   105→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   106→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   107→    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
   108→        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
   109→    ))
   110→    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
   111→
   112→
   113→def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   114→    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
   115→    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
   116→
   117→
   118→def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict, *, score: float = 100.0) -> None:
   119→    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
   120→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   121→        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, score)
   122→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
   123→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   124→    state["strict_score"] = score
   125→    state["overall_score"] = score
   126→    state["objective_score"] = score
   127→
   128→
   129→def _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Mark scored subjective dimensions as fully current/high to clear rerun queue."""
   131→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   132→        assessment = state["subjective_assessments"].setdefault(key, {})
   133→        assessment["placeholder"] = False
   134→        assessment["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   135→        assessment["score"] = 100.0
   136→        dim = state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]]
   137→        dim["score"] = 100.0
   138→        dim["strict"] = 100.0
   139→
   140→
   141→def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
   142→    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
   143→    work_items = state.setdefault("work_items", state.get("issues", {}))
   144→    state["issues"] = work_items
   145→    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
   146→        fid = f"review-{key}"
   147→        work_items[fid] = {
   148→            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
   149→            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
   150→        }
```

```
1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     9→    ScoreDisplayMode,
    10→    plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
    11→    score_display_mode,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import get_plan_start_strict
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.plan_load import warn_plan_load_degraded_once
    18→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import resolve_plan_load_status
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
    20→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
    22→
    23→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _only_run_scan_workflow_remaining(state: dict, plan: dict) -> bool:
    27→    result = build_execution_queue(
    28→        state,
    29→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    30→            status="open",
    31→            count=None,
    32→            plan=plan,
    33→            include_skipped=False,
    34→        ),
    35→    )
    36→    items = result.get("items", [])
    37→    return len(items) == 1 and items[0].get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
    38→
    39→
    40→def scan_queue_preflight(args: object) -> None:
    41→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    42→    # CI profile always passes
    43→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    47→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    48→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    49→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    50→            raise CommandError(
    51→                '--force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    52→                "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    53→                'completion"'
    54→            )
    55→        print(
    56→            colorize(
    57→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    58→                "Queue-destructive reconciliation steps will be skipped.",
    59→                "yellow",
    60→            )
    61→        )
    62→        return
    63→
    64→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan). Use the same
    65→    # plan-load contract as the work queue so degraded-plan handling stays
    66→    # consistent across queue rendering and scan preflight.
    67→    plan_status = resolve_plan_load_status()
    68→    if plan_status.degraded:
    69→        _logger.debug(
    70→            "scan preflight plan load degraded: %s",
    71→            plan_status.error_kind,
    72→        )
    73→        warn_plan_load_degraded_once(
    74→            command_label="scan preflight",
    75→            error_kind=plan_status.error_kind,
    76→            behavior="Queue gating is disabled until the living plan can be loaded again.",
    77→        )
    78→        return
    79→    plan = plan_status.plan
    80→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    81→        return
    82→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    83→        return  # No active cycle
    84→
    85→    # Count plan-aware remaining items.  Block scan when ANY queue items
    86→    # remain (objective OR subjective).  Mid-cycle scans regenerate issue
    87→    # IDs which wipes triage state and re-clusters the queue, undoing
    88→    # prioritisation work.
    89→    try:
    90→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    91→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    92→        plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
    93→        mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
    94→    except OSError:
    95→        _logger.debug("scan preflight queue breakdown skipped", exc_info=True)
    96→        return
    97→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE:
    98→        return  # Queue fully clear or no active cycle — scan allowed
    99→    if (
   100→        mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   101→        and breakdown.queue_total == 1
   102→        and breakdown.workflow == 1
   103→        and _only_run_scan_workflow_remaining(state, plan)
   104→    ):
   105→        return
   106→
   107→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   108→    # GATE — block both FROZEN (objective work) and PHASE_TRANSITION
   109→    # (subjective/workflow items remain)
   110→    raise CommandError(
   111→        f"{remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
   112→        " remaining in your queue.\n"
   113→        "  Scanning mid-cycle regenerates issue IDs and breaks triage state.\n"
   114→        "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.\n\n"
   115→        "  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):\n"
   116→        '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   117→        "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   118→        'completion"'
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   123→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    12→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    14→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    15→    is_synthetic_id,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import load_plan, save_plan
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.scan_issue_reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    21→    mark_postflight_scan_completed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync import (
    24→    ReconcileResult,
    25→    live_planned_queue_empty,
    26→    reconcile_plan,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import current_unscored_ids
    29→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.context import is_mid_cycle
    30→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import clear_score_communicated_sentinel
    31→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_deferred_disposition_item
    32→
    33→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    37→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used."""
    38→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    39→    synthetic = [item for item in order if is_synthetic_id(item)]
    40→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    41→        return False
    42→    for item in synthetic:
    43→        order.remove(item)
    44→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    45→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
    46→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    47→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    48→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    49→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    50→    count = len(synthetic)
    51→    if count:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    55→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    56→                "yellow",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→    return True
    60→
    61→
    62→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    63→    return bool(
    64→        plan.get("queue_order")
    65→        or plan.get("overrides")
    66→        or plan.get("clusters")
    67→        or plan.get("skipped")
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    72→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    73→        return False
    74→    recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    75→    if recon.resurfaced:
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    79→                "cyan",
    80→            )
    81→        )
    82→    return bool(recon.changes)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    86→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
    87→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    88→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
    89→        return False
    90→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
    91→        return False
    92→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    93→    if scores.strict is None:
    94→        return False
    95→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
    96→        "strict": scores.strict,
    97→        "overall": scores.overall,
    98→        "objective": scores.objective,
    99→        "verified": scores.verified,
   100→    }
   101→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   102→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def _has_objective_cycle(
   107→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   108→    plan: dict[str, object],
   109→) -> bool | None:
   110→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   111→    try:
   112→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
   113→
   114→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   115→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   116→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   117→        return None
   118→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   119→
   120→
   121→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   122→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   123→) -> bool:
   124→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   125→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   126→        return False
   127→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   128→        return False
   129→
   130→    try:
   131→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   132→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   133→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   134→            score_display_mode,
   135→        )
   136→
   137→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   138→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   139→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   140→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   141→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   142→        return False
   143→    if not queue_empty:
   144→        return False
   145→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   146→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   147→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   152→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   153→    plan: dict[str, object],
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Record that the cycle's post-review scan has completed."""
   156→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores") and current_unscored_ids(state):
   157→        return False
   158→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   159→        return False
   160→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   161→        plan,
   162→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   167→    plan: dict[str, object],
   168→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   169→) -> bool:
   170→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   171→    if seeded:
   172→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   173→        return True
   174→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   175→    if cleared:
   176→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   177→    return cleared
   178→
   179→
   180→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   181→    plan: dict[str, object],
   182→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   183→) -> bool:
   184→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   185→    if changed:
   186→        append_log_entry(
   187→            plan,
   188→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   189→            actor="system",
   190→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   191→        )
   192→    return changed
   193→
   194→
   195→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   196→    *,
   197→    plan: dict[str, object],
   198→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   199→) -> bool:
   200→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan workflow nudge.
     2→
     3→Dependency-light module that only imports plan/queue helpers — avoids
     4→the import cycle that previously forced function-level imports in scan.py.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    12→    ScoreDisplayMode,
    13→    get_plan_start_strict,
    14→    plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
    15→    score_display_mode,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    18→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    19→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    20→
    21→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→def print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    25→    """Print a queue-count reminder when plan-start scores exist."""
    26→    try:
    27→        plan = load_plan()
    28→        plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
    29→        if plan_start_strict is None:
    30→            return
    31→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    32→        mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
    33→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    34→        _logger.debug("plan workflow nudge skipped", exc_info=True)
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    if mode is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
    38→        return
    39→    queue_total = breakdown.objective_actionable
    40→    print(
    41→        colorize(
    42→            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
    43→            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
    44→            "dim",
    45→        )
    46→    )
    47→
```

```
1→"""Stable normalization helpers for loaded plan payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_semantics import normalize_cluster_semantics
     9→
    10→_HEX_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}$")
    11→
    12→
    13→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
    14→    if old not in d:
    15→        return False
    16→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
    17→    return True
    18→
    19→
    20→def _ensure_container(
    21→    plan: dict[str, Any],
    22→    key: str,
    23→    expected_type: type[list] | type[dict],
    24→    default_factory,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if not isinstance(plan.get(key), expected_type):
    27→        plan[key] = default_factory()
    28→
    29→
    30→def ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    31→    """Normalize top-level container keys onto their runtime shapes."""
    32→    for key, expected_type, default_factory in (
    33→        ("queue_order", list, list),
    34→        ("deferred", list, list),
    35→        ("skipped", dict, dict),
    36→        ("overrides", dict, dict),
    37→        ("clusters", dict, dict),
    38→        ("superseded", dict, dict),
    39→        ("promoted_ids", list, list),
    40→        ("plan_start_scores", dict, dict),
    41→        ("refresh_state", dict, dict),
    42→        ("execution_log", list, list),
    43→        ("epic_triage_meta", dict, dict),
    44→    ):
    45→        _ensure_container(plan, key, expected_type, default_factory)
    46→    _rename_key(plan["epic_triage_meta"], "finding_snapshot_hash", "issue_snapshot_hash")
    47→    _ensure_container(plan, "commit_log", list, list)
    48→    _rename_key(plan, "uncommitted_findings", "uncommitted_issues")
    49→    _ensure_container(plan, "uncommitted_issues", list, list)
    50→    if "commit_tracking_branch" not in plan:
    51→        plan["commit_tracking_branch"] = None
    52→
    53→
    54→def _normalize_cluster_issue_id(raw_id: object) -> str | None:
    55→    if not isinstance(raw_id, str):
    56→        return None
    57→    issue_id = raw_id.strip()
    58→    if not issue_id:
    59→        return None
    60→    if _HEX_SUFFIX_RE.fullmatch(issue_id):
    61→        return None
    62→    if issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::"):
    63→        parts = issue_id.split("::")
    64→        if len(parts) >= 2 and _HEX_SUFFIX_RE.fullmatch(parts[-1]):
    65→            return "::".join(parts[:-1])
    66→    return issue_id
    67→
    68→
    69→def _override_cluster_members(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    70→    members: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    71→    seen_by_cluster: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    72→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    73→    if not isinstance(overrides, dict):
    74→        return members
    75→
    76→    for issue_id, override in overrides.items():
    77→        normalized_issue_id = _normalize_cluster_issue_id(issue_id)
    78→        if normalized_issue_id is None or not isinstance(override, dict):
    79→            continue
    80→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
    81→        if not isinstance(cluster_name, str) or not cluster_name.strip():
    82→            continue
    83→        cluster_name = cluster_name.strip()
    84→        bucket = members.setdefault(cluster_name, [])
    85→        seen = seen_by_cluster.setdefault(cluster_name, set())
    86→        if normalized_issue_id in seen:
    87→            continue
    88→        seen.add(normalized_issue_id)
    89→        bucket.append(normalized_issue_id)
    90→    return members
    91→
    92→
    93→def _execution_log_cluster_members(
    94→    plan: dict[str, Any],
    95→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, str]]:
    96→    members: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    97→    hash_lookup: dict[str, str] = {}
    98→
    99→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   100→        cluster_entry = _execution_log_cluster_entry(entry)
```
