```
    10→from .shared import (
    11→    StageConfirmationRequest,
    12→    ensure_stage_is_confirmable,
    13→    finalize_stage_confirmation,
    14→)
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→from ..stages.records import TriageStages
    17→
    18→# Observe verdicts that trigger auto-skip on confirmation
    19→_AUTO_SKIP_VERDICTS = frozenset({"false positive", "exaggerated"})
    20→
    21→
    22→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = 80
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_referenced_names(text: str, names: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    26→    if not names:
    27→        return []
    28→    return [
    29→        name for name in names
    30→        if name.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or name.lower() in text
    31→    ]
    32→
    33→
    34→def _contains_any(text: str, phrases: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    35→    return any(phrase in text for phrase in phrases)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_phrase_hits(text: str, phrases: tuple[str, ...]) -> int:
    39→    return sum(1 for phrase in phrases if phrase in text)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _validate_observe_attestation(text: str, dimensions: list[str] | None) -> str | None:
    43→    found = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions)
    44→    if found or not dimensions:
    45→        return None
    46→    dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    47→    return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    48→
    49→
    50→def _validate_reflect_attestation(
    51→    text: str,
    52→    *,
    53→    dimensions: list[str] | None,
    54→    cluster_names: list[str] | None,
    55→) -> str | None:
    56→    refs = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions) + _find_referenced_names(text, cluster_names)
    57→    if refs or not (dimensions or cluster_names):
    58→        return None
    59→    return (
    60→        "Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    61→        f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    62→        f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    63→    )
    64→
    65→
    66→def _validate_cluster_attestation(
    67→    text: str,
    68→    *,
    69→    cluster_names: list[str] | None,
    70→    action: str,
    71→    stage: str,
    72→) -> str | None:
    73→    found = _find_referenced_names(text, cluster_names)
    74→    if found or not cluster_names:
    75→        return None
    76→    if stage == "organize":
    77→        if _contains_any(text, ("cluster", "clusters")) and _count_phrase_hits(
    78→            text,
    79→            ("priority", "priorities", "action step", "action steps", "description", "descriptions", "depends-on", "dependency", "dependencies", "issue", "issues", "consolidat"),
    80→        ) >= 2:
    81→            return None
    82→    elif stage == "enrich":
    83→        if _contains_any(text, ("step", "steps", "cluster", "clusters")) and _count_phrase_hits(
    84→            text,
    85→            ("executor-ready", "detail", "details", "file path", "file paths", "issue ref", "issue refs", "effort"),
    86→        ) >= 2:
    87→            return None
    88→    elif stage == "sense-check":
    89→        if _count_phrase_hits(
    90→            text,
    91→            ("content", "structure", "value", "cross-cluster", "dependency", "dependencies", "decision ledger", "enrich-level", "factually accurate"),
    92→        ) >= 2 and _contains_any(text, ("verified", "safe", "pass", "passes", "recorded", "checked")):
    93→            return None
    94→    names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    95→    return (
    96→        f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you {action}, or clearly describe the "
    97→        f"verified {stage} work product. Mention one of: {names}"
    98→    )
    99→
   100→
   101→def validate_attestation(
   102→    attestation: str,
   103→    stage: str,
   104→    *,
   105→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
   106→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
   107→) -> str | None:
   108→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data."""
   109→    text = attestation.lower()
   110→    validators = {
   111→        "observe": lambda: _validate_observe_attestation(text, dimensions),
   112→        "reflect": lambda: _validate_reflect_attestation(
   113→            text,
   114→            dimensions=dimensions,
   115→            cluster_names=cluster_names,
   116→        ),
   117→        "organize": lambda: _validate_cluster_attestation(
   118→            text,
   119→            cluster_names=cluster_names,
   120→            action="organized",
   121→            stage="organize",
   122→        ),
   123→        "enrich": lambda: _validate_cluster_attestation(
   124→            text,
   125→            cluster_names=cluster_names,
   126→            action="enriched",
   127→            stage="enrich",
   128→        ),
   129→        "sense-check": lambda: _validate_cluster_attestation(
   130→            text,
   131→            cluster_names=cluster_names,
   132→            action="sense-checked",
   133→            stage="sense-check",
   134→        ),
   135→    }
   136→    validator = validators.get(stage)
   137→    return validator() if validator is not None else None
   138→
   139→
   140→def _apply_observe_auto_skips(
   141→    plan: dict,
   142→    meta: dict,
   143→    services: TriageServices,
   144→) -> int:
   145→    """Create skip entries for false-positive/exaggerated dispositions.
   146→
   147→    Returns the number of issues auto-skipped. Also updates the disposition
   148→    map with decision/target/decision_source and removes auto-skipped IDs
   149→    from queue_order.
   150→    """
   151→    from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
   152→
   153→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   154→    if not dispositions:
   155→        return 0
   156→
   157→    skipped = plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   158→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   159→    count = 0
   160→
   161→    for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items():
   162→        verdict = disp.get("verdict", "")
   163→        if verdict not in _AUTO_SKIP_VERDICTS:
   164→            continue
   165→        # Don't double-skip
   166→        if issue_id in skipped:
   167→            continue
   168→        skipped[issue_id] = {
   169→            "issue_id": issue_id,
   170→            "kind": "triage_observe_auto",
   171→            "reason": verdict,
   172→            "note": disp.get("verdict_reasoning", ""),
   173→            "created_at": utc_now(),
   174→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   175→        }
   176→        # Update disposition with auto-skip decision
   177→        disp["decision"] = "skip"
   178→        disp["target"] = verdict
   179→        disp["decision_source"] = "observe_auto"
   180→        count += 1
   181→
   182→    # Remove auto-skipped IDs from queue_order
   183→    if count:
   184→        auto_skipped_ids = {
   185→            issue_id for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items()
   186→            if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto"
   187→        }
   188→        plan["queue_order"] = [
   189→            fid for fid in queue_order if fid not in auto_skipped_ids
   190→        ]
   191→        services.save_plan(plan)
   192→
   193→    return count
   194→
   195→
   196→def _undo_observe_auto_skips(plan: dict, meta: dict) -> int:
   197→    """Remove previously auto-skipped entries before a fresh observe run.
   198→
   199→    Returns the number of entries un-skipped.
   200→    """
   201→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   202→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   203→    count = 0
   204→
   205→    # Find all entries with decision_source == "observe_auto"
   206→    auto_skipped_ids = {
   207→        issue_id for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items()
   208→        if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto"
   209→    }
   210→    for issue_id in auto_skipped_ids:
   211→        entry = skipped.get(issue_id)
   212→        if entry and entry.get("kind") == "triage_observe_auto":
   213→            del skipped[issue_id]
   214→            count += 1
   215→
   216→    return count
   217→
   218→
   219→def confirm_observe(
   220→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   221→    plan: dict,
   222→    stages: TriageStages,
   223→    attestation: str | None,
   224→    *,
   225→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   226→) -> None:
   227→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   228→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   229→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="observe"):
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    obs = stages["observe"]
   233→
   234→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Verify queued issues against the code", "bold"))
   235→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
   236→
   237→    by_dim = obs.get("dimension_counts", {})
   238→    dim_names = obs.get("dimension_names", sorted(by_dim))
   239→    issue_count = int(obs.get("issue_count", 0) or 0)
   240→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
   241→    for dim in dim_names:
   242→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} issues")
   243→
   244→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
   245→    if cited:
   246→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} issue IDs in your report.")
   247→
   248→    min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10)) if issue_count > 0 else 0
   249→    if len(cited) < min_citations:
   250→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: only {len(cited)} issue ID(s) cited in report (need {min_citations}+).", "red"))
   251→        print(colorize("  Your observe report should reference specific issues by their hash IDs to prove", "dim"))
   252→        print(colorize("  you actually read them. Cite at least 10% of issues or 5, whichever is smaller.", "dim"))
   253→        print(colorize("  Re-record observe with more issue citations, then re-confirm.", "dim"))
   254→        return
   255→
   256→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   257→        plan=plan,
   258→        stages=stages,
   259→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   260→            stage="observe",
   261→            attestation=attestation,
   262→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   263→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."',
   264→            validation_stage="observe",
   265→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   266→            validation_kwargs={"dimensions": dim_names},
   267→            log_action="triage_confirm_observe",
   268→            not_satisfied_hint="If not, continue reviewing issues before reflecting.",
   269→        ),
   270→        services=resolved_services,
   271→    ):
   272→        return
   273→
   274→    # Auto-skip false-positive/exaggerated issues on observe confirmation
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   276→    auto_skipped = _apply_observe_auto_skips(plan, meta, resolved_services)
   277→    if auto_skipped:
   278→        print(colorize(f"  Auto-skipped {auto_skipped} false-positive/exaggerated issue(s).", "green"))
   279→
   280→    print_user_message(
   281→        "Hey — observe is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   282→        " --stage reflect --report \"...\"` next. No need to reply,"
   283→        " just keep going."
   284→    )
   285→
   286→
   287→def confirm_reflect(
   288→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   289→    plan: dict,
   290→    stages: dict,
   291→    attestation: str | None,
   292→    *,
   293→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   294→) -> None:
   295→    """Show reflect summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   296→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   297→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="reflect"):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   301→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   302→    ref = stages["reflect"]
   303→
   304→    print(colorize("  Stage: REFLECT — Form strategy & present to user", "bold"))
   305→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   306→
   307→    review_issues = getattr(si, "review_issues", getattr(si, "open_issues", {}))
   308→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(review_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   309→    if recurring:
   310→        print(f"  Your strategy identified {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s):")
   311→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   312→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   313→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   314→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   315→            print(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}")
   316→    else:
   317→        print("  No recurring patterns detected.")
   318→
   319→    report = ref.get("report", "")
   320→    if report:
   321→        print()
   322→        print(colorize("  ┌─ Your strategy briefing ───────────────────────┐", "cyan"))
   323→        for line in report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   324→            print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   325→        if len(report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   326→            print(colorize("  │ ...", "cyan"))
   327→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 51 + "┘", "cyan"))
   328→
   329→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe", {})
   330→    observe_dims = list(observe_stage.get("dimension_names", []))
   331→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   332→    reflect_clusters = [name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")]
   333→
   334→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   335→        plan=plan,
   336→        stages=stages,
   337→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   338→            stage="reflect",
   339→            attestation=attestation,
   340→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   341→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect --attestation "My strategy accounts for..."',
   342→            validation_stage="reflect",
   343→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   344→            validation_kwargs={
   345→                "dimensions": reflect_dims,
   346→                "cluster_names": reflect_clusters,
   347→            },
   348→            log_action="triage_confirm_reflect",
   349→            not_satisfied_hint="If not, refine your strategy before organizing.",
   350→        ),
   351→        services=resolved_services,
   352→    ):
   353→        return
   354→    print_user_message(
   355→        "Hey — reflect is confirmed. Now create clusters, enrich"
   356→        " them with action steps, then run `desloppify plan triage"
   357→        " --stage organize --report \"...\"`. No need to reply,"
   358→        " just keep going."
   359→    )
   360→
   361→
   362→__all__ = ["MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN", "confirm_observe", "confirm_reflect", "validate_attestation"]
   363→
```

```
    48→_BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD = 5
    49→_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("reviewed", "not gaming")
    50→_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    51→    "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming "
    52→    "the score by suppressing a real defect."
    53→)
    54→
    55→
    56→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    57→    *,
    58→    kind: str,
    59→    attestation: str | None,
    60→    note: str | None,
    61→) -> bool:
    62→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    63→        return True
    64→    if not validate_attestation(
    65→        attestation,
    66→        required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    67→    ):
    68→        show_attestation_requirement(
    69→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    70→            attestation,
    71→            _TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    72→            required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    73→        )
    74→        return False
    75→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    78→            file=sys.stderr,
    79→        )
    80→        return False
    81→    return True
    82→
    83→
    84→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    85→    *,
    86→    kind: str,
    87→    state_file: Path | None,
    88→    issue_ids: list[str],
    89→    note: str | None,
    90→    attestation: str | None,
    91→) -> dict | None:
    92→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    93→    if status is None:
    94→        return None
    95→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    96→    for fid in issue_ids:
    97→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    98→            state_data,
    99→            fid,
   100→            status,
   101→            note or "",
   102→            attestation=attestation,
   103→        )
   104→    return state_data
   105→
   106→
   107→def _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   108→    *,
   109→    confirm: bool,
   110→    kind: str,
   111→    reason: str | None,
   112→    attestation: str | None,
   113→) -> bool:
   114→    if not confirm or kind != "temporary":
   115→        return True
   116→    if not reason:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  --confirm requires --reason to describe why this is being deferred.",
   120→                "red",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return False
   124→    required_phrases = ("i have actually reflected", "not deferring")
   125→    normalized_attest = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
   126→    missing = [phrase for phrase in required_phrases if phrase not in normalized_attest]
   127→    if not missing:
   128→        return True
   129→    print(
   130→        colorize(
   131→            "  Deferring items requires you to confirm you've thought this through.",
   132→            "yellow",
   133→        )
   134→    )
   135→    print(colorize("  Add this to your command:", "dim"))
   136→    print()
   137→    print(
   138→        colorize(
   139→            '    --attest "I have actually reflected and '
   140→            'I am not deferring this for lazy reasons."',
   141→            "dim",
   142→        )
   143→    )
   144→    print()
   145→    return False
   146→
   147→
   148→def _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids: list[str], *, confirm: bool) -> None:
   149→    if len(issue_ids) <= _BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD:
   150→        return
```

```
   184→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   185→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   186→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   187→    state = runtime.state
   188→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   192→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   193→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   194→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   195→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   196→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   197→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   198→
   199→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   200→
   201→    if not _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   202→        confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)),
   203→        kind=kind,
   204→        reason=reason,
   205→        attestation=attestation,
   206→    ):
   207→        return
   208→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
   209→        raise CommandError(
   210→            "Invalid plan skip attestation or note.",
   211→            exit_code=2,
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   215→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   216→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   217→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   218→    if not issue_ids:
   219→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids, confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)))

   225→        kind=kind,
   226→        state_file=state_file,
   227→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   228→        note=note,
   229→        attestation=attestation,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)

   280→
   281→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   282→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   283→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   284→    state = runtime.state
   285→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   286→        return
   287→
   288→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   289→
   290→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   291→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   292→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   293→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   294→    if not issue_ids:
   295→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   296→        return
   297→
   298→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   299→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = unskip_items(
   300→        plan,
   301→        issue_ids,
   302→        include_protected=include_protected,
   303→    )
   304→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   305→    append_log_entry(
   306→        plan,
   307→        "unskip",
   308→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   309→        actor="user",
   310→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   311→    )
   312→    if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=unskipped_ids, state=state):
   313→        emit_transition_message(LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE)
   314→
   315→    reopened: list[str] = []
   316→    if need_reopen:
   317→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   318→        for fid in need_reopen:
   319→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   320→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   321→            plan=plan,
   322→            plan_path=plan_file,
   323→            state_data=state_data,
   324→            state_path_value=state_file,
   325→        )
   326→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   327→    else:
   328→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   329→
   330→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   331→    if protected_kept:
   332→        print(
   333→            colorize(
   334→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   335→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   336→                "yellow",
   337→            )
   338→        )
   339→
   340→
   341→def cmd_plan_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   342→    """Move deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking entirely."""
   343→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   344→    state = runtime.state
   345→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   346→        return
   347→
   348→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   349→
   350→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   351→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   352→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   353→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   354→    if not issue_ids:
   355→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   356→        return
   357→
   358→    removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)
   359→    if not removed:
   360→        print(colorize("  No matching deferred items found.", "yellow"))
   361→        return
   362→
   363→    # Reopen issues whose state status was set by the skip (deferred/triaged_out)
   364→    # so they don't get stranded with a non-open status while untracked by plan.
   365→    _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {"deferred", "triaged_out"}
   366→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   367→    issues = state_data.get("work_items", {})
   368→    reopen_ids = [
   369→        fid for fid in removed
   370→        if issues.get(fid, {}).get("status") in _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES
   371→    ]
   372→
   373→    if reopen_ids:
   374→        for fid in reopen_ids:
   375→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open")
   376→
   377→    append_log_entry(
   378→        plan,
   379→        "backlog",
   380→        issue_ids=removed,
   381→        actor="user",
   382→    )
   383→    if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=removed, state=state_data):
   384→        emit_transition_message(LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE)
   385→
   386→    if reopen_ids:
   387→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   388→            plan=plan,
   389→            plan_path=plan_file,
   390→            state_data=state_data,
   391→            state_path_value=state_file,
   392→        )
   393→    else:
   394→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   395→
   396→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {len(removed)} item(s) to backlog.", "green"))
   397→    if reopen_ids:
   398→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopen_ids)} deferred/triaged-out issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   399→
```

```
1→"""Display and dashboard rendering for plan triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import build_triage_snapshot
    11→
    12→from .layout import (
    13→    print_action_guidance,
    14→    print_dashboard_header,
    15→    print_issues_by_dimension,
    16→    print_prior_stage_reports,
    17→    show_plan_summary,
    18→)
    19→from .primitives import print_stage_progress
    20→from ..review_coverage import (
    21→    cluster_issue_ids,
    22→    triage_coverage,
    23→)
    24→from ..stage_queue import print_cascade_clear_feedback
    25→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    26→
    27→
    28→def _cluster_tags(cluster: dict) -> str:
    29→    """Build display tags for cluster metadata."""
    30→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    31→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    32→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    33→    tags: list[str] = []
    34→    if auto:
    35→        tags.append("auto")
    36→    if desc:
    37→        tags.append("desc")
    38→    else:
    39→        tags.append("no desc")
    40→    if steps:
    41→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    42→    elif not auto:
    43→        tags.append("no steps")
    44→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _print_active_clusters(active_clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    48→    """Render currently active clusters with tags and descriptions."""
    49→    if not active_clusters:
    50→        return
    51→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    52→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    53→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    54→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    55→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    56→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{_cluster_tags(cluster)}{desc_str}")
    57→
    58→
    59→def _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters: dict, open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    60→    """Return issue IDs not currently assigned to any cluster."""
    61→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    62→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    63→        all_clustered.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    64→    return [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
    65→
    66→
    67→def _print_unclustered_issues(unclustered: list[str], open_issues: dict) -> None:
    68→    """Render unclustered issue list with compact IDs."""
    69→    if not unclustered:
    70→        return
    71→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    72→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
    73→        issue = open_issues[fid]
    74→        detail = issue.get("detail")
    75→        dim = (detail or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    76→        short = short_issue_id(fid)
    77→        print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    78→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
    79→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
    80→
    81→
    82→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    83→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    84→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    85→    active_clusters = {name: c for name, c in clusters.items() if cluster_issue_ids(c)}
    86→    _print_active_clusters(active_clusters)
    87→
    88→    unclustered = _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters, open_issues)
    89→    if unclustered:
    90→        _print_unclustered_issues(unclustered, open_issues)
    91→    elif open_issues:
    92→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
    93→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def print_reflect_result(
    97→    *,
    98→    issue_count: int,
    99→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   100→    recurring: dict,
   101→    report: str,
   102→    is_reuse: bool,
   103→    cleared: list,
   104→    stages: dict,
   105→) -> None:
   106→    """Print reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   107→    print(colorize(f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, {len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).", "green"))
   108→    if is_reuse:
   109→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   110→        if cleared:
   111→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   112→    else:
   113→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   114→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   115→    if recurring_dims:
   116→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   117→            info = recurring[dim]
   118→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open", "dim"))
   119→
   120→    print()
   121→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
   122→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   123→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   124→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   125→    print_user_message(
   126→        "Reflect recorded. Before confirming — check the"
   127→        " subagent's report. Is it a strategy or just observe"
   128→        " restated? It should include a concrete cluster"
   129→        " blueprint: which clusters, which issues, what to skip"
   130→        " (with per-issue reasons). Confirm when the blueprint"
   131→        " is specific enough for organize to execute mechanically."
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→def print_organize_result(
   136→    *,
   137→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    report: str,
   140→    is_reuse: bool,
   141→    cleared: list,
   142→    stages: dict,
   143→) -> None:
   144→    """Print organize stage output including cluster summary and next steps."""
   145→    print(colorize(f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).", "green"))
   146→    if is_reuse:
   147→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   148→        if cleared:
   149→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   150→    else:
   151→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   152→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   153→    for name in manual_clusters:
   154→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   155→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   156→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   157→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   158→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))} issues, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   159→
   160→    print()
   161→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ────────────┐", "cyan"))
   162→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   163→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   164→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   165→    print_user_message(
   166→        "Organize recorded. Before confirming — does the"
   167→        " organize output match the reflect blueprint? Clusters"
   168→        " by file area (same PR), not by theme? Step count <"
   169→        " issue count (consolidated)? Cluster names describe"
   170→        " locations, not problem types? This should read like"
   171→        " a set of PR plans."
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→def print_reflect_dashboard(
   176→    si: object,
   177→    plan: dict,
   178→    *,
   179→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   180→) -> None:
```

   100→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   101→"""
   102→    return f"""\
   103→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   104→
   105→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   106→
   107→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   108→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   109→
   110→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   111→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering/not-worth-it) with reasoning. Use
   112→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   113→
   114→**Important: Issues with verdict `false-positive` or `exaggerated` have already been auto-skipped
   115→by observe confirmation.** They are NOT in your issue set and do NOT need ledger entries.
   116→
   117→For issues with verdict `over-engineering` or `not-worth-it`: observe flagged these as
   118→questionable, but YOU decide. If you agree they're not worth fixing, skip them. If you disagree
   119→and think the fix has value, cluster them. These are judgment calls, not factual determinations.
   120→
   121→### What you must do:
   122→
   123→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   124→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   125→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   126→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? Apply YAGNI: if the fix is more complex than the
   127→   problem, skip it. Valid skip reasons:
   128→   - "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication"
   129→   - "the current code is clear and simple despite being theoretically suboptimal"
   130→   - "observe verdict: not-worth-it — the improvement is marginal"
   131→   - "fixing this requires touching 8 files for a naming consistency issue nobody notices"
   132→   Invalid: "low priority" (everything is low priority compared to something)
   133→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   134→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   135→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   136→   another round of fixes.
   137→7. **Consider mechanical backlog** — the backlog section shows auto-clusters
   138→   (pre-grouped detector findings) and unclustered items. For each auto-cluster:
   139→   - **promote**: name it in a `## Backlog Promotions` section. Prefer clusters with
   140→     `[autofix: ...]` hints because they are lower-risk.
   141→   - **leave**: say nothing. Silence means it stays in backlog.
   142→   - **supersede**: absorb the underlying work into a review cluster when the same files
   143→     or root cause already belong together.
   144→   For unclustered items: promote individually or group related ones into a manual cluster.
   145→   Mechanical items are NOT part of the Coverage Ledger — that ledger remains review-issues only.
   146→8. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   147→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   148→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   149→
   150→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   151→
   152→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   153→```
   154→## Coverage Ledger
   155→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   156→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   157→
   158→## Cluster Blueprint
   159→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   160→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   161→
   162→## Backlog Promotions
   163→- Promote auto/unused-imports (overlaps with the files in cluster "task-typing")
   164→
   165→## Skip Decisions
   166→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   167→```
   168→
   169→### Hard accounting rule
   170→
   171→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   172→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   173→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   174→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   175→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   176→
   177→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   178→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   179→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   180→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   181→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   182→- No `## Coverage Ledger`
   183→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   184→- Missing or duplicated issue hashes
   185→
   186→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   187→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   188→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   189→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   190→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   191→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering), issue U (not-worth-it:
   192→  adds ErrorBoundary wrapper around 3 components that already handle errors inline —
   193→  technically cleaner but doubles the JSX nesting depth for no behavioral change).
   194→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   195→
   196→{tail}
   197→"""
   198→
   199→
   200→def _organize_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   201→    intro = (
   202→        "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   203→        "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   204→        "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   205→        "Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned\n"
   206→        "(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why."
   207→    )
   208→    process_block = """\
   209→2. **False-positive and exaggerated issues have already been auto-skipped** by observe confirmation.
   210→   You do NOT need to skip them manually. If reflect skipped additional issues (over-engineering/not-worth-it),
   211→   skip those now:
   212→   ```
   213→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason from reflect>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
   214→   ```
   215→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   216→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   217→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   218→5. Promote any mechanical backlog items that reflect explicitly selected:
   219→   - Auto-clusters: `desloppify plan promote auto/<cluster-name>`
   220→   - Individual items: `desloppify plan promote <issue-id>`
   221→   - With placement: `desloppify plan promote <pattern> before -t <target>`
   222→6. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   223→7. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   224→8. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   225→"""
   226→    tail = """\
   227→When done, run:
   228→```
   229→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names or the organized work>"
   230→```
   231→"""
   232→    if mode == "output_only":
   233→        intro = (
   234→            "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   235→            "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   236→            "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   237→            "Translate that plan into a precise organize report. If something\n"
   238→            "doesn't work as planned (issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust\n"
   239→            "and document why."
   240→        )
   241→        process_block = """\
   242→2. **False-positive and exaggerated issues have already been auto-skipped** by observe confirmation.
   243→   If reflect skipped additional issues (over-engineering/not-worth-it), include those skip decisions.
   244→3. Define the clusters exactly as they should be created.
   245→4. Assign every kept issue to a cluster.
   246→5. Consolidate steps: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue.
   247→6. Assign an effort level to each planned step (trivial/small/medium/large).
   248→7. Call out cross-cluster dependencies when clusters touch overlapping files.
   249→"""
   250→        tail = """\
   251→When done, write a plain-text organize report that names the clusters, their issue membership,
   252→their consolidated steps, and any skip/dependency decisions. The orchestrator records the stage.
   253→"""
   254→    return f"""\
   255→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   256→
   257→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   258→
   259→{intro}
   260→
   261→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   262→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   263→
   264→### Process
   265→
   266→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint AND the observe assessments table (both provided below)
   267→{process_block}
   268→
   269→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   270→
   271→Before recording, verify:
   272→- [ ] Every issue that reflect marked as skip has been skipped (false-positive/exaggerated are already auto-skipped)
   273→- [ ] No cluster where the total fix effort exceeds the problem severity (KISS: prefer
   274→      simple code with a known smell over complex code with a hidden abstraction)
   275→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   276→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   277→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   278→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   279→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   280→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   281→
   282→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.
   283→
   284→{tail}
   285→"""
   286→
   287→
   288→def _enrich_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   289→    subagent_block = """\
   290→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   291→
   292→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get steps, members, and reviewer suggestions
   293→2. For deeper analysis, run `desloppify show <issue-id> --no-budget` to see full evidence
   294→3. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   295→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   296→4. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   297→   function name), and the exact change to make
   298→5. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   299→"""
   300→    tail = """\
   301→When done, run:
   302→```
   303→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names or the executor-ready work>"
   304→```
   305→"""
   306→    if mode == "output_only":
   307→        subagent_block = """\
   308→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   309→
   310→1. Inspect the cluster definition provided in the prompt context
   311→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   312→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   313→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   314→   function name), and the exact change to make
   315→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   316→"""
   317→        tail = """\
   318→When done, write a plain-text enrichment report describing the corrected step details,
   319→effort tags, and issue refs for each cluster. The orchestrator records the stage.
   320→"""
   321→    return f"""\
   322→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   323→
   324→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   325→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   326→
   327→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   328→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   329→Use the organize report included above as the authoritative cluster plan; do not re-derive
   330→strategy from old triage runs unless you find a concrete mismatch you need to explain.
   331→
   332→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   333→
   334→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   335→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   336→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   337→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   338→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   339→
   340→### How to enrich
   341→
   342→{subagent_block}
   343→
   344→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   345→
   346→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   347→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   348→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   349→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   350→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   351→
   352→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   353→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   354→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   355→
   356→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   357→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   358→
   359→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   360→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   361→
   362→**Over-engineering the fix.** KISS: the fix should be simpler than the problem. If you're
   363→adding interfaces, registries, factory methods, or configuration to fix a code smell, the
   364→cure is worse than the disease. Prefer: delete code > inline code > extract a function >
   365→add an abstraction. If the simplest fix is "delete these 10 lines," say that — don't
   366→propose a refactoring framework.
   367→
   368→### Examples
   369→
   370→**GOOD step detail:**
   371→```
   372→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   373→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   374→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   375→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   376→```
   377→
   378→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   379→```
   380→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   381→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   382→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   383→```
   384→
   385→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   386→
   387→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   388→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   389→
   390→### File path rules
   391→
   392→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   393→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   394→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   395→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   396→
   397→{tail}
   398→"""
   399→
   400→
   401→def _sense_check_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   402→    content_fix_block = (
   403→        'Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`'
   404→    )
   405→    structure_fix_block = """\
   406→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   407→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   408→"""
   409→    value_commands = """\
   410→Use these commands aggressively:
   411→- `desloppify next --count 100` to inspect the current execution queue
   412→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to inspect cluster members and steps
   413→- `desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash> --no-budget` to re-read the underlying finding
   414→- `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --update-step N --detail "..." --effort small`
   415→- `desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<why>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."`
   416→- `desloppify plan cluster delete <name>` if you skipped everything it contained
   417→"""
   418→    tail = """\
   419→When done, run:
   420→```
   421→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<findings summary>"
   422→```
   423→"""
   424→    if mode == "output_only":
   425→        content_fix_block = (
   426→            "Report the exact step corrections that need to be made; the orchestrator will apply them."
   427→        )
   428→        structure_fix_block = (
   429→            "Report the exact dependency additions or cascade steps that need to be made; "
   430→            "the orchestrator will apply them.\n"
   431→        )
   432→        value_commands = (
   433→            "State the exact queue items to keep, tighten, or skip, plus any cluster-step "
   434→            "updates or deletions needed. The orchestrator will apply them."
   435→        )
   436→        tail = """\
   437→When done, write a plain-text sense-check report with concrete content, structure, and value findings.
   438→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   439→"""
   440→    investigation_hint = (
   441→        "Use `desloppify show <issue-hash> --no-budget` to verify step details "
   442→        "match the original reviewer analysis.\n\n"
   443→        if mode == "self_record"
   444→        else ""
   445→    )
   446→    constraints = render_constraints(header="   Also flag steps that:", bullet="   - ")
   447→    return f"""\
   448→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   449→
   450→This stage is handled by three subagents. If you are being run as a
   451→single-subprocess fallback, perform ALL three checks below.
   452→
   453→Execution order: content batches (parallel) → structure batch → value batch (sequential).
   454→Value runs last because it needs the corrected plan state from content and structure.
   455→
   456→### Content Check (per cluster)
   457→{investigation_hint}For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   458→1. OVER-ENGINEERING (check FIRST — if the fix fails this, skip the rest):
   459→   Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   460→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   461→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   462→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   463→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   464→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   465→   - Add more lines of code than they remove (net complexity increase)
   466→{constraints}
   467→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative,
   468→   recommend removing it entirely — don't just flag it, say "skip this cluster."
   469→2. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   470→3. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   471→4. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   472→5. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   473→6. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   474→7. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   475→8. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   476→
   477→{content_fix_block}
   478→
   479→### Structure Check (global)
   480→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   481→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   482→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   483→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   484→
   485→{structure_fix_block}
   486→
   487→### Value Check (global — YAGNI/KISS pass)
   488→
   489→Walk the CURRENT planned work item by item and make the final judgment about value.
   490→Ask: does doing this make the codebase genuinely better? Beauty is a valid reason to keep work,
   491→but not if it buys that beauty with new indirection, wrappers, abstraction layers, or confusion.
   492→
   493→For EVERY live queue target, choose exactly one:
   494→1. `keep` — clearly improves correctness, clarity, cohesion, simplicity, or elegance
   495→2. `tighten` — worth doing, but the plan must be simplified or made more concrete first
   496→3. `skip` — the fix would add churn, indirection, coordination, or abstraction for too little gain
   497→
   498→What should usually be skipped:
   499→- facade pruning that just spreads imports
   500→- abstraction-for-abstraction's-sake
   501→- tiny theoretical cleanups that make the code harder to follow
   502→- fixes whose implementation is more complicated than the current code
   503→
   504→What can still be worth keeping:
   505→- simplifications that delete layers or reduce branching
   506→- aesthetic cleanups that genuinely improve readability without adding machinery
   507→- focused unifications that make naming or flow more coherent with less confusion
   508→
   509→{value_commands}
   510→
   511→Required process:
   512→1. Re-read the actual code behind each queue target.
   513→2. Apply the rubric above, not the raw issue title.
   514→3. Tighten any keeper whose steps are too vague, too broad, or too complicated.
   515→4. Permanently skip anything that fails the value test.
   516→5. Delete dead clusters after skipping all their members.
   517→
   518→Required report structure — include a Decision Ledger section:
   519→```
   520→## Decision Ledger
   521→- cluster-or-id -> keep
   522→- cluster-or-id -> tighten
   523→- cluster-or-id -> skip
   524→```
   525→
   526→{tail}
   527→"""
   528→
   529→
   530→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {

   532→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   533→    "organize": _organize_instructions,

```
    35→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    36→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    37→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    38→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in review_issues)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    42→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    43→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    44→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    45→    for issue in review_issues.values():
    46→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    47→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    48→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    49→    parts = [
    50→        "## Issue Summary",
    51→        f"Open review issues: {len(review_issues)}",
    52→    ]
    53→    if dims:
    54→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    55→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    56→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    57→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    58→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    59→
    60→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    61→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}
    62→    if existing:
    63→        parts.append("")
    64→        parts.append("## Clusters")
    65→        for cname, cdata in existing.items():
    66→            desc = cdata.get("description") or ""
    67→            count = len(cdata.get("issue_ids", []))
    68→            desc_str = f" ({desc})" if desc else ""
    69→            parts.append(f"- {cname}: {count} issues{desc_str}")
    70→
    71→    return "\n".join(parts)
    72→
    73→
    74→_INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS = """\
    75→
    76→## Available Investigation Commands
    77→
    78→To see full detail for a specific issue:
    79→  desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash-pattern> --no-budget
    80→To see all open review issues with suggestions:
    81→  desloppify show review --status open --no-budget
    82→To see a cluster's steps, members, and suggestions:
    83→  desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    84→To list all clusters:
    85→  desloppify plan cluster list --verbose"""
    86→
    87→
    88→def _issue_context_for_stage(
    89→    stage: str,
    90→    triage_input: TriageInput,
    91→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",

    99→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
   100→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
```
